================
File: buildSrc/src/main/groovy/ComponentTestsPlugin.groovy
================
 1: import org.gradle.api.Plugin
 2: import org.gradle.api.Project
 3: import org.gradle.api.tasks.testing.Test
 4: 
 5: class ComponentTestsPlugin implements Plugin<Project> {
 6: 
 7:     @Override
 8:     void apply(Project project) {
 9: 
10:     	project.apply(plugin: 'eclipse')
11: 
12:         project.sourceSets {
13:             componentTest {
14:                 java {
15:                     compileClasspath += main.output + test.output
16:                     runtimeClasspath += main.output + test.output
17:                     srcDir project.file('src/component-test/java')
18:                 }
19:                 resources.srcDir project.file('src/component-test/resources')
20:             }
21:         }
22: 
23:         project.configurations {
24:             componentTestCompile.extendsFrom testCompile
25:             componentTestRuntime.extendsFrom testRuntime
26:         }
27: 
28: 		project.eclipse.classpath.plusConfigurations << project.configurations.componentTestCompile
29: 
30:         project.task("componentTest", type: Test) {
31:             testClassesDirs = project.sourceSets.componentTest.output.classesDirs
32:             classpath = project.sourceSets.componentTest.runtimeClasspath
33:         }
34: 
35:         project.tasks.withType(Test) {
36:             reports.html.destination = project.file("${project.reporting.baseDir}/${name}")
37:         }
38:     }
39: }

================
File: buildSrc/src/main/groovy/FtgoApiDependencyResolverPlugin.groovy
================
 1: import org.gradle.api.Plugin
 2: import org.gradle.api.Project
 3: import org.gradle.api.internal.artifacts.dependencies.DefaultProjectDependency
 4: 
 5: class FtgoApiDependencyResolverPlugin implements Plugin<Project> {
 6: 
 7:     @Override
 8:     void apply(Project project) {
 9: 
10:         project.ext.ftgoApiSpecsDir = "${project.buildDir}/ftgo-api-specs"
11: 
12:         def c = project.configurations.create('ftgoApiSpecification')
13: 
14:         project.configurations.implementation.extendsFrom(c)
15: 
16:         def resolveTask = project.task("ftgoResolveAPIDependencies",
17:                 type: FtgoResolveAPIDependencies,
18:                 group: 'build setup',
19:                 description: "fetch API dependencies")
20: 
21:         project.afterEvaluate {
22:             project.configurations.ftgoApiSpecification.allDependencies.each {
23:                 if (it instanceof DefaultProjectDependency) {
24:                     def buildTask = it.dependencyProject.tasks.getByPath("build")
25:                     resolveTask.dependsOn(buildTask)
26:                 }
27:             }
28:         }
29:     }
30: 
31: }

================
File: buildSrc/src/main/groovy/FtgoJSONSchema2PojoPlugin.groovy
================
 1: import org.gradle.api.Plugin
 2: import org.gradle.api.Project
 3: 
 4: class FtgoJSONSchema2PojoPlugin implements Plugin<Project> {
 5: 
 6:     @Override
 7:     void apply(Project project) {
 8: 
 9:         project.apply(plugin: FtgoApiDependencyResolverPlugin)
10: 
11:         def jsonSchemaSources = project.container(JSONSchemaSource)
12:         project.extensions.add('ftgoJsonSchema2Pojo', jsonSchemaSources)
13: 
14:         jsonSchemaSources.all {
15:             def source = delegate as JSONSchemaSource
16: 
17:             source.codeGen = project.task("ftgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen${source.name.capitalize()}",
18:                     type: FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen,
19:                     group: 'build',
20:                     description: "Code generator") as FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen
21: 
22: 
23:             source.codeGen.targetDirectory = new File(project.buildDir, "generated-js2p-code-${source.name}")
24: 
25:             source.codeGen.dependsOn(project.tasks.ftgoResolveAPIDependencies)
26: 
27:             project.afterEvaluate {
28:                 project.sourceSets[source.sourceSet].java.srcDirs += [source.codeGen.targetDirectory]
29:                 project.tasks.compileJava.dependsOn(source.codeGen)
30:             }
31: 
32: 
33:         }
34:     }
35: 
36: }

================
File: buildSrc/src/main/groovy/FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen.groovy
================
 1: import org.gradle.api.DefaultTask
 2: import org.gradle.api.file.FileCollection
 3: import org.gradle.api.tasks.TaskAction
 4: import org.jsonschema2pojo.GenerationConfig
 5: import org.jsonschema2pojo.Jsonschema2Pojo
 6: import org.jsonschema2pojo.DefaultGenerationConfig
 7: 
 8: class FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen  extends DefaultTask {
 9: 
10:     FileCollection source
11:     String targetPackage;
12:     boolean includeAdditionalProperties;
13:     boolean generateBuilders
14:     boolean useLongIntegers
15:     File targetDirectory
16: 
17:     @TaskAction
18:     def generate() {
19:         GenerationConfig configuration = new DefaultGenerationConfig() {
20: 
21:             @Override
22:             Iterator<URL> getSource() {
23:                 FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen.this.source.collect { it.toURL()}.iterator()
24:             }
25: 
26:             @Override
27:             String getTargetPackage() {
28:                 FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen.this.targetPackage
29:             }
30: 
31:             @Override
32:             boolean isIncludeAdditionalProperties() {
33:                 FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen.this.includeAdditionalProperties
34:             }
35: 
36:             @Override
37:             boolean isGenerateBuilders() {
38:                 FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen.this.generateBuilders
39:             }
40: 
41:             @Override
42:             boolean isUseLongIntegers() {
43:                 FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen.this.useLongIntegers
44:             }
45: 
46:             @Override
47:             File getTargetDirectory() {
48:                 return FtgoJSONSchemaToPojoCodeGen.this.targetDirectory
49:             }
50:         }
51: 
52: 
53:         Jsonschema2Pojo.generate(configuration)
54:     }
55: 
56: 
57: }

================
File: buildSrc/src/main/groovy/FtgoOpenApiCodeGenPlugin.groovy
================
 1: import org.gradle.api.Plugin
 2: import org.gradle.api.Project
 3: 
 4: class FtgoOpenApiCodeGenPlugin implements Plugin<Project> {
 5: 
 6:     @Override
 7:     void apply(Project project) {
 8:         project.pluginManager.apply("org.hidetake.swagger.generator")
 9:         project.apply(plugin: FtgoApiDependencyResolverPlugin)
10: 
11:         project.afterEvaluate {
12:             project.swaggerSources.each {
13:                 def name = it.name
14:                 project.swaggerSources[name].code.dependsOn(project.tasks.getByPath("ftgoResolveAPIDependencies"))
15:                 project.compileJava.dependsOn(project.swaggerSources[name].code)
16:                 project.sourceSets.main.java.srcDir "${project.swaggerSources[name].code.outputDir}/src/main/java"
17:                 project.sourceSets.main.resources.srcDir "${project.swaggerSources[name].code.outputDir}/src/main/resources"
18:             }
19:         }
20: 
21:     }
22: }

================
File: buildSrc/src/main/groovy/FtgoResolveAPIDependencies.groovy
================
 1: import org.gradle.api.tasks.Sync
 2: 
 3: class FtgoResolveAPIDependencies extends Sync {
 4: 
 5:     FtgoResolveAPIDependencies() {
 6: 
 7: 
 8:         from {
 9:             project.configurations.ftgoApiSpecification.resolve().collect {
10:                 project.zipTree(it)
11:             }
12:         }
13:         include("**/*.json")
14:         exclude("**/META-INF/**")
15:         into(project.ext.ftgoApiSpecsDir )
16: 
17:         includeEmptyDirs = false
18: 
19:     }
20: 
21: }

File: buildSrc/src/main/groovy/IntegrationTestsPlugin.groovy
================
 1: import org.gradle.api.Plugin
 2: import org.gradle.api.Project
 3: import org.gradle.api.tasks.testing.Test
 4: 
 5: class IntegrationTestsPlugin implements Plugin<Project> {
 6: 
 7:     @Override
 8:     void apply(Project project) {
 9: 
10:         project.apply(plugin: 'eclipse')
11: 
12:         project.sourceSets {
13:             integrationTest {
14:                 java {
15:                     compileClasspath += main.output + test.output
16:                     runtimeClasspath += main.output + test.output
17:                     srcDir project.file('src/integration-test/java')
18:                 }
19:                 resources.srcDir project.file('src/integration-test/resources')
20:             }
21:         }
22: 
23:         project.configurations {
24:             integrationTestCompile.extendsFrom testCompile
25:             integrationTestRuntime.extendsFrom testRuntime
26:         }
27: 
28:         project.eclipse.classpath.plusConfigurations << project.configurations.integrationTestCompile
29: 
30:         project.task("integrationTest", type: Test) {
31:             testClassesDirs = project.sourceSets.integrationTest.output.classesDirs
32:             classpath = project.sourceSets.integrationTest.runtimeClasspath
33:         }
34: 
35:         project.tasks.withType(Test) {
36:             reports.html.destination = project.file("${project.reporting.baseDir}/${name}")
37:         }
38:     }
39: }

================
File: buildSrc/src/main/groovy/WaitForMySql.java
================
 1: import org.gradle.api.DefaultTask;
 2: import org.gradle.api.tasks.TaskAction;
 3: import java.sql.Connection;
 4: import java.sql.DriverManager;
 5: import java.sql.SQLException;
 6: import java.util.Optional;
 7: import java.util.function.Supplier;
 8: public class WaitForMySql extends DefaultTask {
 9:   @TaskAction
10:   public void waitForMySql() {
11:     System.out.println("Waiting for MySql...");
12:     loadDriver();
13:     waitForConnection();
14:   }
15:   private String getenv(String name, String defaultValue) {
16:     return Optional.ofNullable(System.getenv(name)).orElse(defaultValue);
17:   }
18:   private String getenv(String name, Supplier<String> defaultValue) {
19:     return Optional.ofNullable(System.getenv(name)).orElseGet(defaultValue);
20:   }
21:   private void loadDriver() {
22:     try {
23:       System.out.println("Trying to initialize driver");
24:       String datasourceDriverClassName = getenv("SPRING_DATASOURCE_DRIVER_CLASS_NAME", "com.mysql.jdbc.Driver");
25:       Class.forName(datasourceDriverClassName);
26:       System.out.println("Initialization succeed");
27:     } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
28:       System.out.println("Initialization failed");
29:       throw new RuntimeException(e);
30:     }
31:   }
32:   private void waitForConnection() {
33:     while (true) {
34:       Connection connection = null;
35:       try {
36:         System.out.println("Trying to connect...");
37:         String datasourceUrl = getenv("SPRING_DATASOURCE_URL", () -> String.format("jdbc:mysql://%s/eventuate", getenv("DOCKER_HOST_IP", "localhost")));
38:         String datasourceUsername = getenv("SPRING_DATASOURCE_USERNAME", "mysqluser");
39:         String datasourcePassword = getenv("SPRING_DATASOURCE_PASSWORD", "mysqlpw");
40:         connection = DriverManager.getConnection(datasourceUrl, datasourceUsername, datasourcePassword);
41:         System.out.println("Connection succeed");
42:         break;
43:       } catch (SQLException e) {
44:         System.out.println("Connection failed");
45:         sleep();
46:       } finally {
47:         if (connection != null) {
48:           closeConnection(connection);
49:         }
50:       }
51:     }
52:   }
53:   private void closeConnection(Connection connection) {
54:     try {
55:       System.out.println("Trying to close connection");
56:       connection.close();
57:       System.out.println("Connection closed");
58:     } catch (SQLException e) {
59:       System.out.println("Failed to close connection, see stack trace:");
60:       e.printStackTrace();
61:     }
62:   }
63:   private void sleep() {
64:     System.out.println("sleeping for 5 seconds...");
65:     try {
66:       Thread.sleep(5000);
67:     } catch (InterruptedException ie) {
68:       throw new RuntimeException(ie);
69:     }
70:   }
71: }

================
File: buildSrc/src/main/groovy/WaitForMySqlPlugin.java

1: import org.gradle.api.Plugin;

4:   @Override
5:   public void apply(Project project) {

7:   }
8: }

File: common-swagger/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/eventstore/examples/customersandorders/commonswagger/CommonSwaggerConfiguration.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.eventstore.examples.customersandorders.commonswagger;
 2: import com.fasterxml.classmate.TypeResolver;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;
 4: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 5: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 6: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 7: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
 8: import org.springframework.web.context.request.async.DeferredResult;
 9: import springfox.documentation.builders.RequestHandlerSelectors;
10: import springfox.documentation.schema.WildcardType;
11: import springfox.documentation.spi.DocumentationType;
12: import springfox.documentation.spring.web.plugins.Docket;
13: import springfox.documentation.swagger2.annotations.EnableSwagger2;
14: import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
15: import static springfox.documentation.schema.AlternateTypeRules.newRule;
16: @Configuration
17: @EnableSwagger2
18: public class CommonSwaggerConfiguration {
19:     @Bean
20:     public Docket api() {
21:         return new Docket(DocumentationType.SWAGGER_2)
22:                 .select()
23:                 .apis(RequestHandlerSelectors.basePackage("net.chrisrichardson.ftgo"))
24:                 .build()
25:                 .pathMapping("/")
26:                 .genericModelSubstitutes(ResponseEntity.class, CompletableFuture.class)
27:                 .alternateTypeRules(
28:                         newRule(typeResolver.resolve(DeferredResult.class,
29:                                         typeResolver.resolve(ResponseEntity.class, WildcardType.class)),
30:                                 typeResolver.resolve(WildcardType.class)),
31:                         newRule(Money.class, String.class)
32:                 )
33:                 .useDefaultResponseMessages(false)
34:                 ;
35:     }
36:     @Autowired
37:     private TypeResolver typeResolver;
38: }

================
File: deployment/kubernetes/scripts/kubernetes-wait-for-ready-pods.sh
================
 1: #! /bin/bash
 2: pods=$*
 3: done=false
 4: while [[ "$done" = false ]]; do
 5:         for pod in $pods; do
 6:             outcome=$(kubectl get pod $pod -o=jsonpath='{.status.containerStatuses[0].ready}')
 7:             if [[ "$outcome" == "true" ]]; then
 8:                     done=true
 9:             else
10:                     done=false
11:                     break
12:             fi
13:         done
14:         if [[ "$done" = true ]]; then
15:                 echo connected
16:                 break;
17:         fi
18:         echo -n .
19:         sleep 1
20: done

================
File: deployment/kubernetes/scripts/port-forwards.sh
================
 1: #! /bin/bash -e
 2: #declare -A MYMAP=( ['ftgo-appi-gateway']=8087  )
 3: doforward() {
 4:     service=$1
 5:     port=$2
 6:     remotePort=$3
 7:     pod=$(kubectl get pods --selector=svc=$service  -o jsonpath='{.items[*].metadata.name}')
 8:     echo $service $pod $port
 9:     kubectl port-forward $pod ${port}:${remotePort} &
10:     echo $! > port-forward-${service}.pid
11: }
12: doforward 'ftgo-accounting-service' 8085 8080
13: doforward 'ftgo-consumer-service' 8081 8080
14: doforward 'ftgo-api-gateway' 8087 8080
15: doforward 'ftgo-order-service' 8082 8080
16: doforward 'ftgo-restaurant-service' 8084 8080
17: doforward 'ftgo-kitchen-service' 8083 8080
18: exit 0

4: COPY ftgo-order-history.json .

9: CMD ./wait-for-dynamodblocal.sh && ./create-dynamodb-tables.sh

 2: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.common.Command;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;
 4: public class ReverseAuthorizationCommand implements Command {
 5:   private long consumerId;
 6:   private Long orderId;
 7:   private Money orderTotal;
 8:   private ReverseAuthorizationCommand() {
 9:   }
10:   public ReverseAuthorizationCommand(long consumerId, Long orderId, Money orderTotal) {
11:     this.consumerId = consumerId;
12:     this.orderId = orderId;
13:     this.orderTotal = orderTotal;
14:   }
15:   public long getConsumerId() {
16:     return consumerId;
17:   }
18:   public void setConsumerId(long consumerId) {
19:     this.consumerId = consumerId;
20:   }
21:   public Money getOrderTotal() {
22:     return orderTotal;
23:   }
24:   public void setOrderTotal(Money orderTotal) {
25:     this.orderTotal = orderTotal;
26:   }
27:   public Long getOrderId() {
28:     return orderId;
29:   }

31:     this.orderId = orderId;

 3: org.springframework.cloud.contract.spec.Contract.make {

 9:         }

11:     outputMessage {
12:         sentTo('net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.createorder.CreateOrderSaga-reply')

 5: import java.util.Collections;
 6: import java.util.List;
 7: import static io.eventuate.EventUtil.events;
 8: public class Account extends ReflectiveMutableCommandProcessingAggregate<Account, AccountCommand> {
 9:   public List<Event> process(CreateAccountCommand command) {
10:     return events(new AccountCreatedEvent());
11:   }

13:   }
14:   public List<Event> process(AuthorizeCommandInternal command) {

18:     return Collections.emptyList();
19:   }
20:   public List<Event> process(ReviseAuthorizationCommandInternal command) {
21:     return Collections.emptyList();
22:   }
23:   public void apply(AccountAuthorizedEvent event) {
24:   }
25:   public void apply(SagaReplyRequestedEvent event) {
26:     // TODO - need a way to not need this method
27:   }
28: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountingservice.messaging;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventEnvelope;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlers;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlersBuilder;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountingservice.domain.AccountingService;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.domain.ConsumerCreated;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: public class AccountingEventConsumer {
 9:   @Autowired
10:   private AccountingService accountingService;
11:   public DomainEventHandlers domainEventHandlers() {
12:     return DomainEventHandlersBuilder
13:             .forAggregateType("net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.domain.Consumer")
14:             .onEvent(ConsumerCreated.class, this::createAccount) // TODO this is hack to get the correct package
15:             .build();
16:   }
17:   private void createAccount(DomainEventEnvelope<ConsumerCreated> dee) {
18:     accountingService.create(dee.getAggregateId());
19:   }
20: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountingservice.messaging;
 2: import io.eventuate.javaclient.spring.EnableEventHandlers;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandDispatcher;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandDispatcherFactory;
 5: import io.eventuate.tram.spring.commands.consumer.TramCommandConsumerConfiguration;
 6: import io.eventuate.tram.spring.consumer.jdbc.TransactionalNoopDuplicateMessageDetectorConfiguration;
 7: import io.eventuate.tram.spring.events.subscriber.TramEventSubscriberConfiguration;

 9: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventDispatcherFactory;
10: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.eventsourcingsupport.SagaReplyRequestedEventSubscriber;

12: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountingservice.domain.AccountServiceConfiguration;
13: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.CommonConfiguration;
14: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
15: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
16: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
17: import java.util.Collections;
18: @Configuration
19: @EnableEventHandlers
20: @Import({AccountServiceConfiguration.class, CommonConfiguration.class, TramEventSubscriberConfiguration.class, TramCommandConsumerConfiguration.class, TransactionalNoopDuplicateMessageDetectorConfiguration.class})
21: public class AccountingMessagingConfiguration {

25:   }

32:     return new AccountingServiceCommandHandler();
33:   }

36:                                              AccountServiceChannelConfiguration data, CommandDispatcherFactory commandDispatcherFactory) {
37:     return commandDispatcherFactory.make(data.getCommandDispatcherId(), target.commandHandlers());
38:   }
39:   @Bean
40:   public AccountServiceChannelConfiguration accountServiceChannelConfiguration() {
41:     return new AccountServiceChannelConfiguration("accountCommandDispatcher", "accountCommandChannel");
42:   }
43:   @Bean
44:   public SagaReplyRequestedEventSubscriber sagaReplyRequestedEventSubscriber() {
45:     return new SagaReplyRequestedEventSubscriber("accountingServiceSagaReplyRequestedEventSubscriber", Collections.singleton(Account.class.getName()));
46:   }

================
File: ftgo-accounting-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/accountingservice/messaging/AccountingServiceCommandHandler.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountingservice.messaging;
 2: import io.eventuate.sync.AggregateRepository;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlers;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandMessage;
 5: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.participant.SagaCommandHandlersBuilder;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountingservice.domain.*;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountservice.api.AccountDisabledReply;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountservice.api.AuthorizeCommand;
 9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountservice.api.ReverseAuthorizationCommand;
10: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountservice.api.ReviseAuthorization;
11: import org.slf4j.Logger;
12: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
13: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
14: import static io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlerReplyBuilder.withFailure;
15: import static io.eventuate.tram.sagas.eventsourcingsupport.UpdatingOptionsBuilder.replyingTo;
16: public class AccountingServiceCommandHandler {
17:   private Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
18:   @Autowired
19:   private AggregateRepository<Account, AccountCommand> accountRepository;
20:   public CommandHandlers commandHandlers() {
21:     return SagaCommandHandlersBuilder
22:             .fromChannel("accountingService")
23:             .onMessage(AuthorizeCommand.class, this::authorize)
24:             .onMessage(ReverseAuthorizationCommand.class, this::reverseAuthorization)
25:             .onMessage(ReviseAuthorization.class, this::reviseAuthorization)
26:             .build();
27:   }
28:   public void authorize(CommandMessage<AuthorizeCommand> cm) {
29:     AuthorizeCommand command = cm.getCommand();
30:     accountRepository.update(Long.toString(command.getConsumerId()),
31:             makeAuthorizeCommandInternal(command),
32:             replyingTo(cm)
33:                     .catching(AccountDisabledException.class, () -> withFailure(new AccountDisabledReply()))
34:                     .build());
35:   }
36:   public void reverseAuthorization(CommandMessage<ReverseAuthorizationCommand> cm) {
37:     ReverseAuthorizationCommand command = cm.getCommand();
38:     accountRepository.update(Long.toString(command.getConsumerId()),
39:             makeReverseAuthorizeCommandInternal(command),
40:             replyingTo(cm)
41:                     .catching(AccountDisabledException.class, () -> withFailure(new AccountDisabledReply()))
42:                     .build());
43:   }
44:   public void reviseAuthorization(CommandMessage<ReviseAuthorization> cm) {
45:     ReviseAuthorization command = cm.getCommand();
46:     accountRepository.update(Long.toString(command.getConsumerId()),
47:             makeReviseAuthorizeCommandInternal(command),
48:             replyingTo(cm)
49:                     .catching(AccountDisabledException.class, () -> withFailure(new AccountDisabledReply()))
50:                     .build());
51:   }
52:   private AuthorizeCommandInternal makeAuthorizeCommandInternal(AuthorizeCommand command) {
53:     return new AuthorizeCommandInternal(Long.toString(command.getConsumerId()), Long.toString(command.getOrderId()), command.getOrderTotal());
54:   }
55:   private ReverseAuthorizationCommandInternal makeReverseAuthorizeCommandInternal(ReverseAuthorizationCommand command) {
56:     return new ReverseAuthorizationCommandInternal(Long.toString(command.getConsumerId()), Long.toString(command.getOrderId()), command.getOrderTotal());
57:   }
58:   private ReviseAuthorizationCommandInternal makeReviseAuthorizeCommandInternal(ReviseAuthorization command) {
59:     return new ReviseAuthorizationCommandInternal(Long.toString(command.getConsumerId()), Long.toString(command.getOrderId()), command.getOrderTotal());
60:   }
61: }

File: ftgo-accounting-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/accountingservice/web/AccountsController.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountingservice.web;
 2: import io.eventuate.EntityNotFoundException;
 3: import io.eventuate.sync.AggregateRepository;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountingservice.domain.Account;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountingservice.domain.AccountCommand;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
 8: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
 9: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
10: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
11: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMethod;
12: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
13: @RestController
14: @RequestMapping(path="/accounts")
15: public class AccountsController {
16:   @Autowired
17:   private AggregateRepository<Account, AccountCommand> accountRepository;
18:   @RequestMapping(path="/{accountId}", method= RequestMethod.GET)
19:   public ResponseEntity<GetAccountResponse> getAccount(@PathVariable String accountId) {
20:        try {
21:           return new ResponseEntity<>(new GetAccountResponse(accountId), HttpStatus.OK);
22:        } catch (EntityNotFoundException e) {
23:          return  new ResponseEntity<>(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);
24:        }
25:   }
26: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.orders;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.proxies.AccountingService;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.proxies.DeliveryService;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.proxies.OrderServiceProxy;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.proxies.KitchenService;
 6: import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
 7: import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.route.RouteLocator;
 8: import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.route.builder.RouteLocatorBuilder;
 9: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
10: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
11: import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClient;
12: import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.server.RouterFunction;
13: import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.server.RouterFunctions;
14: import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.server.ServerResponse;
15: import static org.springframework.web.reactive.function.server.RequestPredicates.GET;
16: @Configuration
17: @EnableConfigurationProperties(OrderDestinations.class)
18: public class OrderConfiguration {
19:   @Bean
20:   public RouteLocator orderProxyRouting(RouteLocatorBuilder builder, OrderDestinations orderDestinations) {
21:     return builder.routes()
22:             .route(r -> r.path("/orders").and().method("POST").uri(orderDestinations.getOrderServiceUrl()))
23:             .route(r -> r.path("/orders").and().method("PUT").uri(orderDestinations.getOrderServiceUrl()))
24:             .route(r -> r.path("/orders/**").and().method("POST").uri(orderDestinations.getOrderServiceUrl()))
25:             .route(r -> r.path("/orders/**").and().method("PUT").uri(orderDestinations.getOrderServiceUrl()))
26:             .route(r -> r.path("/orders").and().method("GET").uri(orderDestinations.getOrderHistoryServiceUrl()))
27:             .build();
28:   }
29:   @Bean
30:   public RouterFunction<ServerResponse> orderHandlerRouting(OrderHandlers orderHandlers) {
31:     return RouterFunctions.route(GET("/orders/{orderId}"), orderHandlers::getOrderDetails);
32:   }
33:   @Bean
34:   public OrderHandlers orderHandlers(OrderServiceProxy orderService, KitchenService kitchenService,
35:                                      DeliveryService deliveryService, AccountingService accountingService) {
36:     return new OrderHandlers(orderService, kitchenService, deliveryService, accountingService);
37:   }
38:   @Bean
39:   public WebClient webClient() {
40:     return WebClient.create();
41:   }
42: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.orders;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.proxies.BillInfo;

 8: import org.apache.commons.lang3.builder.ToStringBuilder;
 9: import reactor.util.function.Tuple4;

14:   }
15:   public OrderDetails(OrderInfo orderInfo) {

19:                       Optional<TicketInfo> ticketInfo,
20:                       Optional<DeliveryInfo> deliveryInfo,
21:                       Optional<BillInfo> billInfo) {
22:     this(orderInfo);
23:     System.out.println("FIXME");
24:   }
25:   @Override
26:   public boolean equals(Object o) {
27:     return EqualsBuilder.reflectionEquals(this, o);
28:   }
29:   @Override
30:   public int hashCode() {
31:     return HashCodeBuilder.reflectionHashCode(this);
32:   }
33:   @Override

================
File: ftgo-api-gateway/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/apiagateway/orders/OrderHandlers.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.orders;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.proxies.*;
 3: import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
 4: import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.server.ServerRequest;
 5: import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.server.ServerResponse;
 6: import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;
 7: import reactor.util.function.Tuple4;
 8: import java.util.Optional;
 9: import static org.springframework.web.reactive.function.BodyInserters.fromObject;
10: public class OrderHandlers {
11:   private OrderServiceProxy orderService;
12:   private KitchenService kitchenService;
13:   private DeliveryService deliveryService;
14:   private AccountingService accountingService;
15:   public OrderHandlers(OrderServiceProxy orderService,
16:                        KitchenService kitchenService,
17:                        DeliveryService deliveryService,
18:                        AccountingService accountingService) {
19:     this.orderService = orderService;
20:     this.kitchenService = kitchenService;
21:     this.deliveryService = deliveryService;
22:     this.accountingService = accountingService;
23:   }
24:   public Mono<ServerResponse> getOrderDetails(ServerRequest serverRequest) {
25:     String orderId = serverRequest.pathVariable("orderId");
26:     Mono<OrderInfo> orderInfo = orderService.findOrderById(orderId);
27:     Mono<Optional<TicketInfo>> ticketInfo = kitchenService
28:             .findTicketById(orderId)
29:             .map(Optional::of)
30:             .onErrorReturn(Optional.empty());
31:     Mono<Optional<DeliveryInfo>> deliveryInfo = deliveryService
32:             .findDeliveryByOrderId(orderId)
33:             .map(Optional::of)
34:             .onErrorReturn(Optional.empty());
35:     Mono<Optional<BillInfo>> billInfo = accountingService
36:             .findBillByOrderId(orderId)
37:             .map(Optional::of)
38:             .onErrorReturn(Optional.empty());
39:     Mono<Tuple4<OrderInfo, Optional<TicketInfo>, Optional<DeliveryInfo>, Optional<BillInfo>>> combined =
40:             Mono.zip(orderInfo, ticketInfo, deliveryInfo, billInfo);
41:     Mono<OrderDetails> orderDetails = combined.map(OrderDetails::makeOrderDetails);
42:     return orderDetails.flatMap(od -> ServerResponse.ok()
43:             .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
44:             .body(fromObject(od)))
45:             .onErrorResume(OrderNotFoundException.class, e -> ServerResponse.notFound().build());
46:   }
47: }

================
File: ftgo-api-gateway/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/apiagateway/proxies/OrderServiceProxy.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.proxies;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.apiagateway.orders.OrderDestinations;
 3: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 4: import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.ClientResponse;
 5: import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClient;
 6: import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;
 7: @Service
 8: public class OrderServiceProxy {
 9:   private OrderDestinations orderDestinations;
10:   private WebClient client;
11:   public OrderServiceProxy(OrderDestinations orderDestinations, WebClient client) {
12:     this.orderDestinations = orderDestinations;
13:     this.client = client;
14:   }
15:   public Mono<OrderInfo> findOrderById(String orderId) {
16:     Mono<ClientResponse> response = client
17:             .get()
18:             .uri(orderDestinations.getOrderServiceUrl() + "/orders/{orderId}", orderId)
19:             .exchange();
20:     return response.flatMap(resp -> {
21:       switch (resp.statusCode()) {
22:         case OK:
23:           return resp.bodyToMono(OrderInfo.class);
24:         case NOT_FOUND:
25:           return Mono.error(new OrderNotFoundException());
26:         default:
27:           return Mono.error(new RuntimeException("Unknown" + resp.statusCode()));
28:       }
29:     });
30:   }
31: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common;
 2: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.SerializationFeature;
 3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.datatype.jsr310.JavaTimeModule;
 4: import io.eventuate.common.json.mapper.JSonMapper;
 5: import javax.annotation.PostConstruct;
 6: public class CommonJsonMapperInitializer {
 7:   @PostConstruct
 8:   public void initialize() {
 9:     registerMoneyModule();
10:   }
11:   public static void registerMoneyModule() {
12:     JSonMapper.objectMapper.registerModule(new MoneyModule());
13:     JSonMapper.objectMapper.registerModule(new JavaTimeModule());
14:     JSonMapper.objectMapper.disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS);
15:   }

File: ftgo-common/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/common/Money.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common;
 2: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.EqualsBuilder;
 3: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.HashCodeBuilder;
 4: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.ToStringBuilder;
 5: import java.math.BigDecimal;
 6: //@Embeddable
 7: //@Access(AccessType.FIELD)
 8: public class Money {
 9:   public static Money ZERO = new Money(0);
10:   private BigDecimal amount;
11:   private Money() {
12:   }
13:   public Money(BigDecimal amount) {
14:     this.amount = amount;
15:   }
16:   public Money(String s) {
17:     this.amount = new BigDecimal(s);
18:   }
19:   public Money(int i) {
20:     this.amount = new BigDecimal(i);
21:   }
22:   @Override
23:   public boolean equals(Object o) {
24:     if (this == o) return true;
25:     if (o == null || getClass() != o.getClass()) return false;
26:     Money money = (Money) o;
27:     return new EqualsBuilder()
28:             .append(amount, money.amount)
29:             .isEquals();
30:   }
31:   @Override
32:   public int hashCode() {
33:     return new HashCodeBuilder(17, 37)
34:             .append(amount)
35:             .toHashCode();
36:   }
37:   @Override
38:   public String toString() {
39:     return new ToStringBuilder(this)
40:             .append("amount", amount)
41:             .toString();
42:   }
43:   public Money add(Money delta) {
44:     return new Money(amount.add(delta.amount));
45:   }
46:   public boolean isGreaterThanOrEqual(Money other) {
47:     return amount.compareTo(other.amount) >= 0;
48:   }
49:   public String asString() {
50:     return amount.toPlainString();
51:   }
52:   public Money multiply(int x) {
53:     return new Money(amount.multiply(new BigDecimal(x)));
54:   }
55:   public Long asLong() {
56:     return multiply(100).amount.longValue();
57:   }
58: }

================
File: ftgo-common/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/common/MoneyModule.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common;
 2: import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonGenerator;
 3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonParser;
 4: import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException;
 5: import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonToken;
 6: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.DeserializationContext;
 7: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.SerializerProvider;
 8: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.deser.std.StdScalarDeserializer;
 9: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.module.SimpleModule;
10: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ser.std.StdScalarSerializer;
11: import java.io.IOException;
12: public class MoneyModule extends SimpleModule {
13:   class MoneyDeserializer extends StdScalarDeserializer<Money> {
14:     protected MoneyDeserializer() {
15:       super(Money.class);
16:     }
17:     @Override
18:     public Money deserialize(JsonParser jp, DeserializationContext ctxt) throws IOException, JsonProcessingException {
19:       JsonToken token = jp.getCurrentToken();
20:       if (token == JsonToken.VALUE_STRING) {
21:         String str = jp.getText().trim();
22:         if (str.isEmpty())
23:           return null;
24:         else
25:           return new Money(str);
26:       } else
27:         throw ctxt.mappingException(getValueClass());
28:     }
29:   }
30:   class MoneySerializer extends StdScalarSerializer<Money> {
31:     public MoneySerializer() {
32:       super(Money.class);
33:     }
34:     @Override
35:     public void serialize(Money value, JsonGenerator jgen, SerializerProvider provider) throws IOException {
36:       jgen.writeString(value.asString());
37:     }
38:   }
39:     @Override
40:   public String getModuleName() {
41:     return "FtgoCommonMOdule";
42:   }
43:   public MoneyModule() {
44:     addDeserializer(Money.class, new MoneyDeserializer());
45:     addSerializer(Money.class, new MoneySerializer());
46:   }
47: }

 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;
 4: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.EqualsBuilder;
 5: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.HashCodeBuilder;
 6: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.ToStringBuilder;
 7: public class ValidateOrderByConsumer implements Command {
 8:   private long consumerId;
 9:   private long orderId;
10:   private Money orderTotal;
11:   private ValidateOrderByConsumer() {
12:   }
13:   @Override
14:   public boolean equals(Object o) {
15:     return EqualsBuilder.reflectionEquals(this, o);
16:   }
17:   @Override
18:   public int hashCode() {
19:     return HashCodeBuilder.reflectionHashCode(this);
20:   }
21:   @Override
22:   public String toString() {
23:     return ToStringBuilder.reflectionToString(this);
24:   }
25:   public ValidateOrderByConsumer(long consumerId, long orderId, Money orderTotal) {
26:     this.consumerId = consumerId;
27:     this.orderId = orderId;
28:     this.orderTotal = orderTotal;
29:   }

31:     return orderId;
32:   }

28:   }

30:     return id;
31:   }
32:   public PersonName getName() {
33:     return name;
34:   }

36:     return new ResultWithEvents<>(new Consumer(name), new ConsumerCreated());
37:   }

================
File: ftgo-consumer-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/consumerservice/domain/ConsumerService.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.domain;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.publisher.DomainEventPublisher;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.publisher.ResultWithEvents;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.PersonName;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
 8: import java.util.Optional;
 9: public class ConsumerService {
10:   @Autowired
11:   private ConsumerRepository consumerRepository;
12:   @Autowired
13:   private DomainEventPublisher domainEventPublisher;
14:   public void validateOrderForConsumer(long consumerId, Money orderTotal) {
15:     Optional<Consumer> consumer = consumerRepository.findById(consumerId);
16:     consumer.orElseThrow(ConsumerNotFoundException::new).validateOrderByConsumer(orderTotal);
17:   }
18:   @Transactional
19:   public ResultWithEvents<Consumer> create(PersonName name) {
20:     ResultWithEvents<Consumer> rwe = Consumer.create(name);
21:     consumerRepository.save(rwe.result);
22:     domainEventPublisher.publish(Consumer.class, rwe.result.getId(), rwe.events);
23:     return rwe;
24:   }
25:   public Optional<Consumer> findById(long consumerId) {
26:     return consumerRepository.findById(consumerId);
27:   }
28: }

================
File: ftgo-consumer-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/consumerservice/domain/ConsumerServiceCommandHandlers.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.domain;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlers;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandMessage;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.messaging.common.Message;
 5: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.participant.SagaCommandHandlersBuilder;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.api.ValidateOrderByConsumer;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: import static io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlerReplyBuilder.withFailure;
 9: import static io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlerReplyBuilder.withSuccess;
10: public class ConsumerServiceCommandHandlers  {
11:   @Autowired
12:   private ConsumerService consumerService;
13:   public CommandHandlers commandHandlers() {
14:     return SagaCommandHandlersBuilder
15:               .fromChannel("consumerService")
16:               .onMessage(ValidateOrderByConsumer.class, this::validateOrderForConsumer)
17:               .build();
18:   }
19:   private Message validateOrderForConsumer(CommandMessage<ValidateOrderByConsumer> cm) {
20:     try {
21:       consumerService.validateOrderForConsumer(cm.getCommand().getConsumerId(), cm.getCommand().getOrderTotal());
22:       return withSuccess();
23:     } catch (ConsumerVerificationFailedException e) {
24:       return withFailure();
25:     }
26:   }
27: }

File: ftgo-consumer-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/consumerservice/domain/ConsumerServiceConfiguration.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.domain;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandDispatcher;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.spring.events.publisher.TramEventsPublisherConfiguration;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.participant.SagaCommandDispatcherFactory;
 5: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.spring.participant.SagaParticipantConfiguration;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.CommonConfiguration;
 7: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.EnableAutoConfiguration;
 8: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 9: import org.springframework.context.annotation.ComponentScan;
10: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
11: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
12: import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.config.EnableJpaRepositories;
13: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.EnableTransactionManagement;
14: @Configuration
15: @EnableJpaRepositories
16: @EnableAutoConfiguration
17: @Import({SagaParticipantConfiguration.class, TramEventsPublisherConfiguration.class, CommonConfiguration.class, SagaParticipantConfiguration.class})
18: @EnableTransactionManagement
19: @ComponentScan
20: public class ConsumerServiceConfiguration {
21:   @Bean
22:   public ConsumerServiceCommandHandlers consumerServiceCommandHandlers() {
23:     return new ConsumerServiceCommandHandlers();
24:   }
25:   @Bean
26:   public ConsumerService consumerService() {
27:     return new ConsumerService();
28:   }
29:   @Bean
30:   public CommandDispatcher commandDispatcher(ConsumerServiceCommandHandlers consumerServiceCommandHandlers, SagaCommandDispatcherFactory sagaCommandDispatcherFactory) {
31:     return sagaCommandDispatcherFactory.make("consumerServiceDispatcher", consumerServiceCommandHandlers.commandHandlers());
32:   }
33: }

File: ftgo-consumer-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/consumerservice/web/ConsumerController.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.web;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.publisher.ResultWithEvents;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.domain.Consumer;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.domain.ConsumerService;
 5: import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
 6: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
 8: @RestController
 9: @RequestMapping(path="/consumers")
10: public class ConsumerController {
11:   private ConsumerService consumerService;
12:   public ConsumerController(ConsumerService consumerService) {
13:     this.consumerService = consumerService;
14:   }
15:   @RequestMapping(method= RequestMethod.POST)
16:   public CreateConsumerResponse create(@RequestBody CreateConsumerRequest request) {
17:     ResultWithEvents<Consumer> result = consumerService.create(request.getName());
18:     return new CreateConsumerResponse(result.result.getId());
19:   }
20:   @RequestMapping(method= RequestMethod.GET,  path="/{consumerId}")
21:   public ResponseEntity<GetConsumerResponse> get(@PathVariable long consumerId) {
22:     return consumerService.findById(consumerId)
23:             .map(consumer -> new ResponseEntity<>(new GetConsumerResponse(consumer.getName()), HttpStatus.OK))
24:             .orElseGet(() -> new ResponseEntity<>(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND));
25:   }
26: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.api.web.DeliveryActionType;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Address;
 4: import javax.persistence.Embeddable;
 5: import javax.persistence.EnumType;
 6: import javax.persistence.Enumerated;
 7: import java.time.LocalDateTime;
 8: @Embeddable
 9: public class Action {
10:   @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
11:   private DeliveryActionType type;
12:   private Address address;
13:   private LocalDateTime time;
14:   protected long deliveryId;
15:   private Action() {
16:   }
17:   public Action(DeliveryActionType type, long deliveryId, Address address, LocalDateTime time) {
18:     this.type = type;
19:     this.deliveryId = deliveryId;
20:     this.address = address;
21:     this.time = time;
22:   }
23:   public boolean actionFor(long deliveryId) {
24:     return this.deliveryId == deliveryId;
25:   }
26:   public static Action makePickup(long deliveryId, Address pickupAddress, LocalDateTime pickupTime) {
27:     return new Action(DeliveryActionType.PICKUP, deliveryId, pickupAddress, pickupTime);
28:   }
29:   public static Action makeDropoff(long deliveryId, Address deliveryAddress, LocalDateTime deliveryTime) {
30:     return new Action(DeliveryActionType.DROPOFF, deliveryId, deliveryAddress, deliveryTime);
31:   }
32:   public DeliveryActionType getType() {
33:     return type;
34:   }
35:   public Address getAddress() {
36:     return address;
37:   }
38: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain;
 2: import javax.persistence.*;
 3: import java.util.List;
 4: @Entity
 5: @Access(AccessType.FIELD)
 6: public class Courier {
 7:   @Id
 8:   private long id;
 9:   @Embedded
10:   private Plan plan;
11:   private Boolean available;
12:   private Courier() {
13:   }
14:   public Courier(long courierId) {
15:     this.id = courierId;
16:     this.plan = new Plan();
17:   }
18:   public static Courier create(long courierId) {
19:     return new Courier(courierId);
20:   }
21:   public void noteAvailable() {
22:     this.available = true;
23:   }
24:   public void addAction(Action action) {
25:     plan.add(action);
26:   }
27:   public void cancelDelivery(long deliveryId) {
28:     plan.removeDelivery(deliveryId);
29:   }
30:   public boolean isAvailable() {
31:     return available;
32:   }
33:   public Plan getPlan() {
34:     return plan;
35:   }
36:   public long getId() {
37:     return id;
38:   }
39:   public void noteUnavailable() {
40:     this.available = false;
41:   }
42:   public List<Action> actionsForDelivery(long deliveryId) {
43:     return plan.actionsForDelivery(deliveryId);
44:   }
45: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain;
 2: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 3: import javax.persistence.EntityManager;
 4: import java.util.List;
 5: public class CustomCourierRepositoryImpl implements CustomCourierRepository {
 6:   @Autowired
 7:   private EntityManager entityManager;
 8: //  @Override
 9: //  public List<Courier> findAllAvailable() {
10: //    return entityManager.createQuery("").getResultList();
11: //  }
12:   @Override
13:   public Courier findOrCreateCourier(long courierId) {
14:     Courier courier = entityManager.find(Courier.class, courierId);
15:     if (courier == null) {
16:       courier = Courier.create(courierId);
17:       entityManager.persist(courier);
18:     }
19:     return courier;
20:   }
21: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Address;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.api.web.DeliveryState;
 4: import javax.persistence.*;
 5: import java.time.LocalDateTime;
 6: @Entity
 7: @Access(AccessType.FIELD)
 8: public class Delivery {
 9:   @Id
10:   private Long id;
11:   @Embedded
12:   @AttributeOverrides({
13:           @AttributeOverride(name="street1", column = @Column(name="pickup_street1")),
14:           @AttributeOverride(name="street2", column = @Column(name="pickup_street2")),
15:           @AttributeOverride(name="city", column = @Column(name="pickup_city")),
16:           @AttributeOverride(name="state", column = @Column(name="pickup_state")),
17:           @AttributeOverride(name="zip", column = @Column(name="pickup_zip")),
18:   }
19:   )
20:   private Address pickupAddress;
21:   @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
22:   private DeliveryState state;
23:   private long restaurantId;
24:   private LocalDateTime pickUpTime;
25:   @Embedded
26:   @AttributeOverrides({
27:           @AttributeOverride(name="street1", column = @Column(name="delivery_street1")),
28:           @AttributeOverride(name="street2", column = @Column(name="delivery_street2")),
29:           @AttributeOverride(name="city", column = @Column(name="delivery_city")),
30:           @AttributeOverride(name="state", column = @Column(name="delivery_state")),
31:           @AttributeOverride(name="zip", column = @Column(name="delivery_zip")),
32:   }
33:   )
34:   private Address deliveryAddress;
35:   private LocalDateTime deliveryTime;
36:   private Long assignedCourier;
37:   private LocalDateTime readyBy;
38:   private Delivery() {
39:   }
40:   public Delivery(long orderId, long restaurantId, Address pickupAddress, Address deliveryAddress) {
41:     this.id = orderId;
42:     this.pickupAddress = pickupAddress;
43:     this.state = DeliveryState.PENDING;
44:     this.restaurantId = restaurantId;
45:     this.deliveryAddress = deliveryAddress;
46:   }
47:   public static Delivery create(long orderId, long restaurantId, Address pickupAddress, Address deliveryAddress) {
48:     return new Delivery(orderId, restaurantId, pickupAddress, deliveryAddress);
49:   }
50:   public void schedule(LocalDateTime readyBy, long assignedCourier) {
51:     this.readyBy = readyBy;
52:     this.assignedCourier = assignedCourier;
53:     this.state = DeliveryState.SCHEDULED;
54:   }
55:   public void cancel() {
56:     this.state = DeliveryState.CANCELLED;
57:     this.assignedCourier = null;
58:   }
59:   public long getId() {
60:     return id;
61:   }
62:   public long getRestaurantId() {
63:     return restaurantId;
64:   }
65:   public Address getDeliveryAddress() {
66:     return deliveryAddress;
67:   }
68:   public Address getPickupAddress() {
69:     return pickupAddress;
70:   }
71:   public DeliveryState getState() {
72:     return state;
73:   }
74:   public Long getAssignedCourier() {
75:     return assignedCourier;
76:   }
77: }

File: ftgo-delivery-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/deliveryservice/domain/DeliveryService.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Address;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.api.web.ActionInfo;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.api.web.DeliveryInfo;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.api.web.DeliveryStatus;
 6: import org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException;
 7: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
 8: import java.time.LocalDateTime;
 9: import java.util.Collections;
10: import java.util.List;
11: import java.util.Optional;
12: import java.util.Random;
13: import java.util.stream.Collectors;
14: public class DeliveryService {
15:   private RestaurantRepository restaurantRepository;
16:   private DeliveryRepository deliveryRepository;
17:   private CourierRepository courierRepository;
18:   private Random random = new Random();
19:   public DeliveryService(RestaurantRepository restaurantRepository, DeliveryRepository deliveryRepository, CourierRepository courierRepository) {
20:     this.restaurantRepository = restaurantRepository;
21:     this.deliveryRepository = deliveryRepository;
22:     this.courierRepository = courierRepository;
23:   }
24:   public void createRestaurant(long restaurantId, String restaurantName, Address address) {
25:     restaurantRepository.save(Restaurant.create(restaurantId, restaurantName, address));
26:   }
27:   public void createDelivery(long orderId, long restaurantId, Address deliveryAddress) {
28:     Restaurant restaurant = restaurantRepository.findById(restaurantId).get();
29:     deliveryRepository.save(Delivery.create(orderId, restaurantId, restaurant.getAddress(), deliveryAddress));
30:   }
31:   public void scheduleDelivery(long orderId, LocalDateTime readyBy) {
32:     Delivery delivery = deliveryRepository.findById(orderId).get();
33:     // Stupid implementation
34:     List<Courier> couriers = courierRepository.findAllAvailable();
35:     Courier courier = couriers.get(random.nextInt(couriers.size()));
36:     courier.addAction(Action.makePickup(delivery.getId(), delivery.getPickupAddress(), readyBy));
37:     courier.addAction(Action.makeDropoff(delivery.getId(), delivery.getDeliveryAddress(), readyBy.plusMinutes(30)));
38:     delivery.schedule(readyBy, courier.getId());
39:   }
40:   public void cancelDelivery(long orderId) {
41:     Delivery delivery = deliveryRepository.findById(orderId).get();
42:     Long assignedCourierId = delivery.getAssignedCourier();
43:     delivery.cancel();
44:     if (assignedCourierId != null) {
45:       Courier courier = courierRepository.findById(assignedCourierId).get();
46:       courier.cancelDelivery(delivery.getId());
47:     }
48:   }
49:   // notePickedUp
50:   // noteDelivered
51:   // noteLocation
52:   void noteAvailable(long courierId) {
53:     courierRepository.findOrCreateCourier(courierId).noteAvailable();
54:   }
55:   void noteUnavailable(long courierId) {
56:     courierRepository.findOrCreateCourier(courierId).noteUnavailable();
57:   }
58:   private Courier findOrCreateCourier(long courierId) {
59:     Courier courier = Courier.create(courierId);
60:     try {
61:       return courierRepository.save(courier);
62:     } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
63:       return courierRepository.findById(courierId).get();
64:     }
65:   }
66:   @Transactional
67:   public void updateAvailability(long courierId, boolean available) {
68:     if (available)
69:       noteAvailable(courierId);
70:     else
71:       noteUnavailable(courierId);
72:   }
73:   // getCourierRoute()
74:   @Transactional
75:   public Optional<DeliveryStatus> getDeliveryInfo(long deliveryId) {
76:     return deliveryRepository.findById(deliveryId).map(delivery -> {
77:       Long assignedCourier = delivery.getAssignedCourier();
78:       List<Action> courierActions = Collections.EMPTY_LIST;
79:       if (assignedCourier != null) {
80:         Courier courier = courierRepository.findById(assignedCourier).get();
81:         courierActions = courier.actionsForDelivery(deliveryId);
82:       }
83:       return makeDeliveryStatus(delivery, assignedCourier, courierActions);
84:     });
85:   }
86:   private DeliveryStatus makeDeliveryStatus(Delivery delivery, Long assignedCourier, List<Action> courierActions) {
87:     return new DeliveryStatus(makeDeliveryInfo(delivery), assignedCourier, courierActions.stream().map(action -> makeActionInfo(action)).collect(Collectors.toList()));
88:   }
89:   private DeliveryInfo makeDeliveryInfo(Delivery delivery) {
90:     return new DeliveryInfo(delivery.getId(), delivery.getState());
91:   }
92:   private ActionInfo makeActionInfo(Action action) {
93:     return new ActionInfo(action.getType());
94:   }
95: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain;

File: ftgo-delivery-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/deliveryservice/domain/Plan.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain;
 2: import javax.persistence.ElementCollection;
 3: import java.util.LinkedList;
 4: import java.util.List;
 5: import java.util.stream.Collectors;
 6: public class Plan {
 7:   @ElementCollection
 8:   private List<Action> actions = new LinkedList<>();
 9:   public void add(Action action) {
10:     actions.add(action);
11:   }
12:   public void removeDelivery(long deliveryId) {
13:     actions = actions.stream().filter(action -> !action.actionFor(deliveryId)).collect(Collectors.toList());
14:   }
15:   public List<Action> getActions() {
16:     return actions;
17:   }
18:   public List<Action> actionsForDelivery(long deliveryId) {
19:     return actions.stream().filter(action -> action.actionFor(deliveryId)).collect(Collectors.toList());
20:   }
21: }

File: ftgo-delivery-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/deliveryservice/messaging/DeliveryMessageHandlers.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.messaging;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventEnvelope;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlers;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlersBuilder;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Address;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain.DeliveryService;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.KitchenServiceChannels;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.events.TicketAcceptedEvent;
 9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.events.TicketCancelled;
10: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.OrderServiceChannels;
11: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderCreatedEvent;
12: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.RestaurantServiceChannels;
13: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.RestaurantCreated;
14: import java.time.LocalDateTime;
15: public class DeliveryMessageHandlers {
16:   private DeliveryService deliveryService;
17:   public DeliveryMessageHandlers(DeliveryService deliveryService) {
18:     this.deliveryService = deliveryService;
19:   }
20:   public DomainEventHandlers domainEventHandlers() {
21:     return DomainEventHandlersBuilder
22:             .forAggregateType(KitchenServiceChannels.TICKET_EVENT_CHANNEL)
23:             .onEvent(TicketAcceptedEvent.class, this::handleTicketAcceptedEvent)
24:             .onEvent(TicketCancelled.class, this::handleTicketCancelledEvent)
25:             .andForAggregateType(OrderServiceChannels.ORDER_EVENT_CHANNEL)
26:             .onEvent(OrderCreatedEvent.class, this::handleOrderCreatedEvent)
27:             .andForAggregateType(RestaurantServiceChannels.RESTAURANT_EVENT_CHANNEL)
28:             .onEvent(RestaurantCreated.class, this::handleRestaurantCreated)
29:             .build();
30:   }
31:   public void handleRestaurantCreated(DomainEventEnvelope<RestaurantCreated> dee) {
32:     Address address = RestaurantEventMapper.toAddress(dee.getEvent().getAddress());
33:     deliveryService.createRestaurant(Long.parseLong(dee.getAggregateId()), dee.getEvent().getName(), address);
34:   }
35:   public void handleOrderCreatedEvent(DomainEventEnvelope<OrderCreatedEvent> dee) {
36:     Address address = dee.getEvent().getDeliveryAddress();
37:     deliveryService.createDelivery(Long.parseLong(dee.getAggregateId()),
38:             dee.getEvent().getOrderDetails().getRestaurantId(), address);
39:   }
40:   public void handleTicketAcceptedEvent(DomainEventEnvelope<TicketAcceptedEvent> dee) {
41:     LocalDateTime readyBy = dee.getEvent().getReadyBy();
42:     deliveryService.scheduleDelivery(Long.parseLong(dee.getAggregateId()), readyBy);
43:   }
44:   public void handleTicketCancelledEvent(DomainEventEnvelope<TicketCancelled> dee) {
45:     deliveryService.cancelDelivery(Long.parseLong(dee.getAggregateId()));
46:   }
47: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.messaging;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.spring.events.subscriber.TramEventSubscriberConfiguration;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventDispatcher;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventDispatcherFactory;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.CommonConfiguration;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain.DeliveryService;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain.DeliveryServiceDomainConfiguration;
 8: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 9: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
10: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
11: @Configuration
12: @Import({DeliveryServiceDomainConfiguration.class, TramEventSubscriberConfiguration.class, CommonConfiguration.class})
13: public class DeliveryServiceMessagingConfiguration {
14:   @Bean
15:   public DeliveryMessageHandlers deliveryMessageHandlers(DeliveryService deliveryService) {
16:     return new DeliveryMessageHandlers(deliveryService);
17:   }
18:   @Bean
19:   public DomainEventDispatcher domainEventDispatcher(DeliveryMessageHandlers deliveryMessageHandlers, DomainEventDispatcherFactory domainEventDispatcherFactory) {
20:     return domainEventDispatcherFactory.make("deliveryService-deliveryMessageHandlers", deliveryMessageHandlers.domainEventHandlers());
21:   }
22: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.messaging;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Address;
 3: public class RestaurantEventMapper {
 4:   public static Address toAddress(net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.Address address) {
 5:     return new Address(address.getStreet1(), address.getStreet2(), address.getCity(), address.getState(), address.getZip());
 6:   }
 7:   public static net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.Address fromAddress(net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Address a) {
 8:     return new net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.Address().withStreet1(a.getStreet1()).withStreet2(a.getStreet2()).withCity(a.getCity()).withZip(a.getZip());
 9:   }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.web;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.api.web.CourierAvailability;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain.DeliveryService;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.api.web.DeliveryStatus;
 5: import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
 6: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
 8: @RestController
 9: public class DeliveryServiceController {
10:   private DeliveryService deliveryService;
11:   public DeliveryServiceController(DeliveryService deliveryService) {
12:     this.deliveryService = deliveryService;
13:   }
14:   @RequestMapping(path="/couriers/{courierId}/availability", method= RequestMethod.POST)
15:   public void updateCourierLocation(@PathVariable long courierId, @RequestBody CourierAvailability availability) {
16:     deliveryService.updateAvailability(courierId, availability.isAvailable());
17:   }
18:   @RequestMapping(path="/deliveries/{deliveryId}", method= RequestMethod.GET)
19:   public ResponseEntity<DeliveryStatus> getDeliveryStatus(@PathVariable long deliveryId) {
20:     return deliveryService.getDeliveryInfo(deliveryId).map(ds -> new ResponseEntity<>(ds, HttpStatus.OK)).orElseGet(() -> new ResponseEntity<>(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND));
21:   }
22: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.web;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.CommonConfiguration;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.deliveryservice.domain.DeliveryServiceDomainConfiguration;

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.aggregates.ResultWithDomainEvents;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.RevisedOrderLineItem;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.TicketDetails;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.events.TicketDomainEvent;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
 8: import java.time.LocalDateTime;
 9: import java.util.List;
10: import java.util.Map;
11: public class KitchenService {
12:   @Autowired
13:   private TicketRepository ticketRepository;
14:   @Autowired
15:   private TicketDomainEventPublisher domainEventPublisher;
16:   @Autowired
17:   private RestaurantRepository restaurantRepository;
18:   public void createMenu(long id, RestaurantMenu menu) {
19:     Restaurant restaurant = new Restaurant(id, menu.getMenuItems());
20:     restaurantRepository.save(restaurant);
21:   }
22:   public void reviseMenu(long ticketId, RestaurantMenu revisedMenu) {
23:     Restaurant restaurant = restaurantRepository.findById(ticketId)
24:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
25:     restaurant.reviseMenu(revisedMenu);
26:   }
27:   public Ticket createTicket(long restaurantId, Long ticketId, TicketDetails ticketDetails) {
28:     ResultWithDomainEvents<Ticket, TicketDomainEvent> rwe = Ticket.create(restaurantId, ticketId, ticketDetails);
29:     ticketRepository.save(rwe.result);
30:     domainEventPublisher.publish(rwe.result, rwe.events);
31:     return rwe.result;
32:   }
33:   @Transactional
34:   public void accept(long ticketId, LocalDateTime readyBy) {
35:     Ticket ticket = ticketRepository.findById(ticketId)
36:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
37:     List<TicketDomainEvent> events = ticket.accept(readyBy);
38:     domainEventPublisher.publish(ticket, events);
39:   }
40:   public void confirmCreateTicket(Long ticketId) {
41:     Ticket ro = ticketRepository.findById(ticketId)
42:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
43:     List<TicketDomainEvent> events = ro.confirmCreate();
44:     domainEventPublisher.publish(ro, events);
45:   }
46:   public void cancelCreateTicket(Long ticketId) {
47:     Ticket ro = ticketRepository.findById(ticketId)
48:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
49:     List<TicketDomainEvent> events = ro.cancelCreate();
50:     domainEventPublisher.publish(ro, events);
51:   }
52:   public void cancelTicket(long restaurantId, long ticketId) {
53:     Ticket ticket = ticketRepository.findById(ticketId)
54:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
55:     // TODO - verify restaurant id
56:     List<TicketDomainEvent> events = ticket.cancel();
57:     domainEventPublisher.publish(ticket, events);
58:   }
59:   public void confirmCancelTicket(long restaurantId, long ticketId) {
60:     Ticket ticket = ticketRepository.findById(ticketId)
61:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
62:     // TODO - verify restaurant id
63:     List<TicketDomainEvent> events = ticket.confirmCancel();
64:     domainEventPublisher.publish(ticket, events);
65:   }
66:   public void undoCancel(long restaurantId, long ticketId) {
67:     Ticket ticket = ticketRepository.findById(ticketId)
68:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
69:     // TODO - verify restaurant id
70:     List<TicketDomainEvent> events = ticket.undoCancel();
71:     domainEventPublisher.publish(ticket, events);
72:   }
73:   public void beginReviseOrder(long restaurantId, Long ticketId, List<RevisedOrderLineItem> revisedOrderLineItems) {
74:     Ticket ticket = ticketRepository.findById(ticketId)
75:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
76:     // TODO - verify restaurant id
77:     List<TicketDomainEvent> events = ticket.beginReviseOrder(revisedOrderLineItems);
78:     domainEventPublisher.publish(ticket, events);
79:   }
80:   public void undoBeginReviseOrder(long restaurantId, Long ticketId) {
81:     Ticket ticket = ticketRepository.findById(ticketId)
82:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
83:     // TODO - verify restaurant id
84:     List<TicketDomainEvent> events = ticket.undoBeginReviseOrder();
85:     domainEventPublisher.publish(ticket, events);
86:   }
87:   public void confirmReviseTicket(long restaurantId, long ticketId, List<RevisedOrderLineItem> revisedOrderLineItems) {
88:     Ticket ticket = ticketRepository.findById(ticketId)
89:             .orElseThrow(() -> new TicketNotFoundException(ticketId));
90:     // TODO - verify restaurant id
91:     List<TicketDomainEvent> events = ticket.confirmReviseTicket(revisedOrderLineItems);
92:     domainEventPublisher.publish(ticket, events);
93:   }
94:   // ...
95: }

26:   @Override

File: ftgo-kitchen-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/kitchenservice/domain/Ticket.java
================
  1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain;
  2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.aggregates.ResultWithDomainEvents;
  3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.NotYetImplementedException;
  4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.RevisedOrderLineItem;
  5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.UnsupportedStateTransitionException;
  6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.TicketDetails;
  7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.TicketLineItem;
  8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.events.TicketAcceptedEvent;
  9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.events.TicketCancelled;
 10: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.events.TicketDomainEvent;
 11: import javax.persistence.*;
 12: import java.time.LocalDateTime;
 13: import java.util.List;
 14: import java.util.Map;
 15: import static java.util.Collections.emptyList;
 16: import static java.util.Collections.singletonList;
 17: @Entity
 18: @Table(name = "tickets")
 19: @Access(AccessType.FIELD)
 20: public class Ticket {
 21:   @Id
 22:   private Long id;
 23:   @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
 24:   private TicketState state;
 25:   private TicketState previousState;
 26:   private Long restaurantId;
 27:   @ElementCollection
 28:   @CollectionTable(name = "ticket_line_items")
 29:   private List<TicketLineItem> lineItems;
 30:   private LocalDateTime readyBy;
 31:   private LocalDateTime acceptTime;
 32:   private LocalDateTime preparingTime;
 33:   private LocalDateTime pickedUpTime;
 34:   private LocalDateTime readyForPickupTime;
 35:   public static ResultWithDomainEvents<Ticket, TicketDomainEvent> create(long restaurantId, Long id, TicketDetails details) {
 36:     return new ResultWithDomainEvents<>(new Ticket(restaurantId, id, details));
 37:   }
 38:   private Ticket() {
 39:   }
 40:   public Ticket(long restaurantId, Long id, TicketDetails details) {
 41:     this.restaurantId = restaurantId;
 42:     this.id = id;
 43:     this.state = TicketState.CREATE_PENDING;
 44:     this.lineItems = details.getLineItems();
 45:   }
 46:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> confirmCreate() {
 47:     switch (state) {
 48:       case CREATE_PENDING:
 49:         state = TicketState.AWAITING_ACCEPTANCE;
 50:         return singletonList(new TicketCreatedEvent(id, new TicketDetails()));
 51:       default:
 52:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
 53:     }
 54:   }
 55:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> cancelCreate() {
 56:     throw new NotYetImplementedException();
 57:   }
 58:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> accept(LocalDateTime readyBy) {
 59:     switch (state) {
 60:       case AWAITING_ACCEPTANCE:
 61:         // Verify that readyBy is in the futurestate = TicketState.ACCEPTED;
 62:         this.acceptTime = LocalDateTime.now();
 63:         if (!acceptTime.isBefore(readyBy))
 64:           throw new IllegalArgumentException(String.format("readyBy %s is not after now %s", readyBy, acceptTime));
 65:         this.readyBy = readyBy;
 66:         return singletonList(new TicketAcceptedEvent(readyBy));
 67:       default:
 68:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
 69:     }
 70:   }
 71:   // TODO reject()
 72:   // TODO cancel()
 73:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> preparing() {
 74:     switch (state) {
 75:       case ACCEPTED:
 76:         this.state = TicketState.PREPARING;
 77:         this.preparingTime = LocalDateTime.now();
 78:         return singletonList(new TicketPreparationStartedEvent());
 79:       default:
 80:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
 81:     }
 82:   }
 83:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> readyForPickup() {
 84:     switch (state) {
 85:       case PREPARING:
 86:         this.state = TicketState.READY_FOR_PICKUP;
 87:         this.readyForPickupTime = LocalDateTime.now();
 88:         return singletonList(new TicketPreparationCompletedEvent());
 89:       default:
 90:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
 91:     }
 92:   }
 93:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> pickedUp() {
 94:     switch (state) {
 95:       case READY_FOR_PICKUP:
 96:         this.state = TicketState.PICKED_UP;
 97:         this.pickedUpTime = LocalDateTime.now();
 98:         return singletonList(new TicketPickedUpEvent());
 99:       default:
100:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
101:     }
102:   }
103:   public void changeLineItemQuantity() {
104:     switch (state) {
105:       case AWAITING_ACCEPTANCE:
106:         // TODO
107:         break;
108:       case PREPARING:
109:         // TODO - too late
110:         break;
111:       default:
112:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
113:     }
114:   }
115:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> cancel() {
116:     switch (state) {
117:       case AWAITING_ACCEPTANCE:
118:       case ACCEPTED:
119:         this.previousState = state;
120:         this.state = TicketState.CANCEL_PENDING;
121:         return emptyList();
122:       default:
123:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
124:     }
125:   }
126:   public Long getId() {
127:     return id;
128:   }
129:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> confirmCancel() {
130:     switch (state) {
131:       case CANCEL_PENDING:
132:         this.state = TicketState.CANCELLED;
133:         return singletonList(new TicketCancelled());
134:       default:
135:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
136:     }
137:   }
138:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> undoCancel() {
139:     switch (state) {
140:       case CANCEL_PENDING:
141:         this.state = this.previousState;
142:         return emptyList();
143:       default:
144:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
145:     }
146:   }
147:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> beginReviseOrder(List<RevisedOrderLineItem> revisedOrderLineItems) {
148:     switch (state) {
149:       case AWAITING_ACCEPTANCE:
150:       case ACCEPTED:
151:         this.previousState = state;
152:         this.state = TicketState.REVISION_PENDING;
153:         return emptyList();
154:       default:
155:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
156:     }
157:   }
158:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> undoBeginReviseOrder() {
159:     switch (state) {
160:       case REVISION_PENDING:
161:         this.state = this.previousState;
162:         return emptyList();
163:       default:
164:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
165:     }
166:   }
167:   public List<TicketDomainEvent> confirmReviseTicket(List<RevisedOrderLineItem> revisedOrderLineItems) {
168:     switch (state) {
169:       case REVISION_PENDING:
170:         this.state = this.previousState;
171:         return singletonList(new TicketRevised());
172:       default:
173:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
174:     }
175:   }
176: }
