================
File: ftgo-kitchen-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/kitchenservice/messagehandlers/KitchenServiceCommandHandler.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.messagehandlers;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlers;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandMessage;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.messaging.common.Message;
 5: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.participant.SagaCommandHandlersBuilder;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.*;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain.RestaurantDetailsVerificationException;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain.Ticket;
 9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain.KitchenService;
10: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
11: import static io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlerReplyBuilder.withFailure;
12: import static io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlerReplyBuilder.withSuccess;
13: import static io.eventuate.tram.sagas.participant.SagaReplyMessageBuilder.withLock;
14: public class KitchenServiceCommandHandler {
15:   @Autowired
16:   private KitchenService kitchenService;
17:   public CommandHandlers commandHandlers() {
18:     return SagaCommandHandlersBuilder
19:             .fromChannel(KitchenServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
20:             .onMessage(CreateTicket.class, this::createTicket)
21:             .onMessage(ConfirmCreateTicket.class, this::confirmCreateTicket)
22:             .onMessage(CancelCreateTicket.class, this::cancelCreateTicket)
23:             .onMessage(BeginCancelTicketCommand.class, this::beginCancelTicket)
24:             .onMessage(ConfirmCancelTicketCommand.class, this::confirmCancelTicket)
25:             .onMessage(UndoBeginCancelTicketCommand.class, this::undoBeginCancelTicket)
26:             .onMessage(BeginReviseTicketCommand.class, this::beginReviseTicket)
27:             .onMessage(UndoBeginReviseTicketCommand.class, this::undoBeginReviseTicket)
28:             .onMessage(ConfirmReviseTicketCommand.class, this::confirmReviseTicket)
29:             .build();
30:   }
31:   private Message createTicket(CommandMessage<CreateTicket>
32:                                                 cm) {
33:     CreateTicket command = cm.getCommand();
34:     long restaurantId = command.getRestaurantId();
35:     Long ticketId = command.getOrderId();
36:     TicketDetails ticketDetails = command.getTicketDetails();
37:     try {
38:       Ticket ticket = kitchenService.createTicket(restaurantId, ticketId, ticketDetails);
39:       CreateTicketReply reply = new CreateTicketReply(ticket.getId());
40:       return withLock(Ticket.class, ticket.getId()).withSuccess(reply);
41:     } catch (RestaurantDetailsVerificationException e) {
42:       return withFailure();
43:     }
44:   }
45:   private Message confirmCreateTicket
46:           (CommandMessage<ConfirmCreateTicket> cm) {
47:     Long ticketId = cm.getCommand().getTicketId();
48:     kitchenService.confirmCreateTicket(ticketId);
49:     return withSuccess();
50:   }
51:   private Message cancelCreateTicket
52:           (CommandMessage<CancelCreateTicket> cm) {
53:     Long ticketId = cm.getCommand().getTicketId();
54:     kitchenService.cancelCreateTicket(ticketId);
55:     return withSuccess();
56:   }
57:   private Message beginCancelTicket(CommandMessage<BeginCancelTicketCommand> cm) {
58:     kitchenService.cancelTicket(cm.getCommand().getRestaurantId(), cm.getCommand().getOrderId());
59:     return withSuccess();
60:   }
61:   private Message confirmCancelTicket(CommandMessage<ConfirmCancelTicketCommand> cm) {
62:     kitchenService.confirmCancelTicket(cm.getCommand().getRestaurantId(), cm.getCommand().getOrderId());
63:     return withSuccess();
64:   }
65:   private Message undoBeginCancelTicket(CommandMessage<UndoBeginCancelTicketCommand> cm) {
66:     kitchenService.undoCancel(cm.getCommand().getRestaurantId(), cm.getCommand().getOrderId());
67:     return withSuccess();
68:   }
69:   public Message beginReviseTicket(CommandMessage<BeginReviseTicketCommand> cm) {
70:     kitchenService.beginReviseOrder(cm.getCommand().getRestaurantId(), cm.getCommand().getOrderId(), cm.getCommand().getRevisedOrderLineItems());
71:     return withSuccess();
72:   }
73:   public Message undoBeginReviseTicket(CommandMessage<UndoBeginReviseTicketCommand> cm) {
74:     kitchenService.undoBeginReviseOrder(cm.getCommand().getRestaurantId(), cm.getCommand().getOrderId());
75:     return withSuccess();
76:   }
77:   public Message confirmReviseTicket(CommandMessage<ConfirmReviseTicketCommand> cm) {
78:     kitchenService.confirmReviseTicket(cm.getCommand().getRestaurantId(), cm.getCommand().getOrderId(), cm.getCommand().getRevisedOrderLineItems());
79:     return withSuccess();
80:   }
81: }

================
File: ftgo-kitchen-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/kitchenservice/messagehandlers/KitchenServiceEventConsumer.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.messagehandlers;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventEnvelope;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlers;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlersBuilder;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain.KitchenService;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain.RestaurantMenu;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.RestaurantCreated;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.RestaurantMenuRevised;
 9: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
10: public class KitchenServiceEventConsumer {
11:   @Autowired
12:   private KitchenService kitchenService;
13:   public DomainEventHandlers domainEventHandlers() {
14:     return DomainEventHandlersBuilder
15:             .forAggregateType("net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.Restaurant")
16:             .onEvent(RestaurantCreated.class, this::createMenu)
17:             .onEvent(RestaurantMenuRevised.class, this::reviseMenu)
18:             .build();
19:   }
20:   private void createMenu(DomainEventEnvelope<RestaurantCreated> de) {
21:     String restaurantIds = de.getAggregateId();
22:     long id = Long.parseLong(restaurantIds);
23:     RestaurantMenu menu = new RestaurantMenu(RestaurantEventMapper.toMenuItems(de.getEvent().getMenu().getMenuItems()));
24:     kitchenService.createMenu(id, menu);
25:   }
26:   public void reviseMenu(DomainEventEnvelope<RestaurantMenuRevised> de) {
27:     long id = Long.parseLong(de.getAggregateId());
28:     RestaurantMenu menu = new RestaurantMenu(RestaurantEventMapper.toMenuItems(de.getEvent().getMenu().getMenuItems()));
29:     kitchenService.reviseMenu(id, menu);
30:   }
31: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.messagehandlers;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.spring.events.subscriber.TramEventSubscriberConfiguration;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventDispatcher;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventDispatcherFactory;
 5: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.participant.SagaCommandDispatcher;
 6: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.participant.SagaCommandDispatcherFactory;
 7: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.spring.participant.SagaParticipantConfiguration;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.CommonConfiguration;
 9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain.KitchenDomainConfiguration;
10: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
11: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
12: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
13: @Configuration
14: @Import({KitchenDomainConfiguration.class, SagaParticipantConfiguration.class, CommonConfiguration.class, TramEventSubscriberConfiguration.class, SagaParticipantConfiguration.class})
15: public class KitchenServiceMessageHandlersConfiguration {

23:   }

27:   }
28:   @Bean
29:   public DomainEventDispatcher domainEventDispatcher(KitchenServiceEventConsumer kitchenServiceEventConsumer, DomainEventDispatcherFactory domainEventDispatcherFactory) {
30:     return domainEventDispatcherFactory.make("kitchenServiceEvents", kitchenServiceEventConsumer.domainEventHandlers());
31:   }
32: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.messagehandlers;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.MenuItem;
 4: import java.util.List;
 5: import java.util.stream.Collectors;
 6: public class RestaurantEventMapper {
 7:   public static List<net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain.MenuItem> toMenuItems(List<MenuItem> menuItems) {
 8:     return menuItems.stream().map(mi -> new net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.domain.MenuItem(mi.getId(), mi.getName(), new Money(mi.getPrice()))).collect(Collectors.toList());
 9:   }
10: }

 6: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
 8: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
 9: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMethod;
10: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
11: @RestController
12: @RequestMapping(path = "/restaurants")
13: public class RestaurantController {
14:   @Autowired
15:   private RestaurantRepository restaurantRepository;

17:   public ResponseEntity<GetRestaurantResponse> getRestaurant(@PathVariable long restaurantId) {
18:     return restaurantRepository.findById(restaurantId)
19:             .map(restaurant -> new ResponseEntity<>(new GetRestaurantResponse(restaurantId), HttpStatus.OK))
20:             .orElseGet( () -> new ResponseEntity<>(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND));
21:   }
22: }

File: ftgo-order-history-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/cqrs/orderhistory/dynamodb/Expressions.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.dynamodb;
 2: import org.apache.commons.lang.StringUtils;
 3: import java.util.Optional;
 4: public class Expressions {
 5:   static String and(String s1, Optional<String> s2) {
 6:     return s2.map(x -> and(s1, x)).orElse(s1);
 7:   }
 8:   static String and(String s1, String s2) {
 9:     if (StringUtils.isBlank(s1))
10:       return s2;
11:     if (StringUtils.isBlank(s2))
12:       return s1;
13:     return String.format("(%s) AND (%s)", s1, s2);
14:   }
15:   static String or(String s1, String s2) {
16:     if (StringUtils.isBlank(s1))
17:       return s2;
18:     if (StringUtils.isBlank(s2))
19:       return s1;
20:     return String.format("(%s) AND (%s)", s1, s2);
21:   }
22: }

 76:                     .map())

 79:   }
 80:   private boolean idempotentUpdate(UpdateItemSpec spec, Optional<SourceEvent>
 81:           eventSource) {
 82:     try {
 83:       table.updateItem(eventSource.map(es -> es.addDuplicateDetection(spec))
 84:               .orElse(spec));
 85:       return true;
 86:     } catch (ConditionalCheckFailedException e) {
 87:       logger.error("not updated {}", eventSource);
 88:       // Do nothing
 89:       return false;
 90:     }
 91:   }

120:   private Set mapKeywords(Order order) {
121:     Set<String> keywords = new HashSet<>();
122:     keywords.addAll(tokenize(order.getRestaurantName()));
123:     keywords.addAll(tokenize(order.getLineItems().stream().map
124:             (OrderLineItem::getName).collect(toList())));
125:     return keywords;
126:   }
127:   private Set<String> tokenize(Collection<String> text) {
128:     return text.stream().flatMap(t -> tokenize(t).stream()).collect(toSet());
129:   }
130:   private Set<String> tokenize(String text) {
131:     Set<String> result = new HashSet<>();
132:     BreakIterator bi = BreakIterator.getWordInstance();
133:     bi.setText(text);
134:     int lastIndex = bi.first();
135:     while (lastIndex != BreakIterator.DONE) {
136:       int firstIndex = lastIndex;
137:       lastIndex = bi.next();
138:       if (lastIndex != BreakIterator.DONE
139:               && Character.isLetterOrDigit(text.charAt(firstIndex))) {
140:         String word = text.substring(firstIndex, lastIndex);
141:         result.add(word);
142:       }
143:     }
144:     return result;
145:   }
146:   private List mapLineItems(List<OrderLineItem> lineItems) {
147:     return lineItems.stream().map(this::mapOrderLineItem).collect(toList());
148:   }

172:   @Override
173:   public OrderHistory findOrderHistory(String consumerId, OrderHistoryFilter
174:           filter) {
175:     QuerySpec spec = new QuerySpec()
176:             .withScanIndexForward(false)
177:             .withHashKey("consumerId", consumerId)
178:             .withRangeKeyCondition(new RangeKeyCondition("creationDate").gt
179:                     (filter.getSince().getMillis()));
180:     filter.getStartKeyToken().ifPresent(token -> spec.withExclusiveStartKey
181:             (toStartingPrimaryKey(token)));
182:     Map<String, Object> valuesMap = new HashMap<>();
183:     String filterExpression = Expressions.and(
184:             keywordFilterExpression(valuesMap, filter.getKeywords()),
185:             statusFilterExpression(valuesMap, filter.getStatus()));
186:     if (!valuesMap.isEmpty())
187:       spec.withValueMap(valuesMap);
188:     if (StringUtils.isNotBlank(filterExpression)) {
189:       spec.withFilterExpression(filterExpression);
190:     }
191:     System.out.print("filterExpression.toString()=" + filterExpression);
192:     filter.getPageSize().ifPresent(spec::withMaxResultSize);
193:     ItemCollection<QueryOutcome> result = index.query(spec);
194:     return new OrderHistory(StreamSupport.stream(result.spliterator(), false)
195:             .map(this::toOrder).collect(toList()),
196:             Optional.ofNullable(result.getLastLowLevelResult().getQueryResult
197:                     ().getLastEvaluatedKey()).map(this::toStartKeyToken));
198:   }
199:   private PrimaryKey toStartingPrimaryKey(String token) {
200:     ObjectMapper om = new ObjectMapper();
201:     Map<String, Object> map;
202:     try {
203:       map = om.readValue(token, Map.class);
204:     } catch (IOException e) {
205:       throw new RuntimeException();
206:     }
207:     PrimaryKey pk = new PrimaryKey();
208:     map.entrySet().forEach(key -> {
209:       pk.addComponent(key.getKey(), key.getValue());
210:     });
211:     return pk;
212:   }
213:   private String toStartKeyToken(Map<String, AttributeValue> lastEvaluatedKey) {
214:     Map<String, Object> map = new HashMap<>();
215:     lastEvaluatedKey.entrySet().forEach(entry -> {
216:       String value = entry.getValue().getS();
217:       if (value == null) {
218:         value = entry.getValue().getN();
219:         map.put(entry.getKey(), Long.parseLong(value));
220:       } else {
221:         map.put(entry.getKey(), value);
222:       }
223:     });
224:     ObjectMapper om = new ObjectMapper();
225:     try {
226:       return om.writeValueAsString(map);
227:     } catch (JsonProcessingException e) {
228:       throw new RuntimeException();
229:     }
230:   }
231:   private Optional<String> statusFilterExpression(Map<String, Object>
232:                                                           expressionAttributeValuesBuilder, Optional<OrderState> status) {
233:     return status.map(s -> {
234:       expressionAttributeValuesBuilder.put(":orderStatus", s.toString());
235:       return "orderStatus = :orderStatus";
236:     });
237:   }
238:   private String keywordFilterExpression(Map<String, Object>
239:                                                  expressionAttributeValuesBuilder, Set<String> kw) {
240:     Set<String> keywords = tokenize(kw);
241:     if (keywords.isEmpty()) {
242:       return "";
243:     }
244:     String cuisinesExpression = "";
245:     int idx = 0;
246:     for (String cuisine : keywords) {
247:       String var = ":keyword" + idx;
248:       String cuisineExpression = String.format("contains(keywords, %s)", var);
249:       cuisinesExpression = Expressions.or(cuisinesExpression, cuisineExpression);
250:       expressionAttributeValuesBuilder.put(var, cuisine);
251:     }
252:     return cuisinesExpression;
253:   }

307:   @Override
308:   public boolean updateOrderState(String orderId, OrderState newState, Optional<SourceEvent> eventSource) {
309:     UpdateItemSpec spec = new UpdateItemSpec()
310:             .withPrimaryKey("orderId", orderId)
311:             .withUpdateExpression("SET #orderStatus = :orderStatus")
312:             .withNameMap(Collections.singletonMap("#orderStatus",
313:                     ORDER_STATUS_FIELD))
314:             .withValueMap(Collections.singletonMap(":orderStatus", newState.toString()))
315:             .withReturnValues(ReturnValue.NONE);
316:     return idempotentUpdate(spec, eventSource);
317:   }

340:   }
341:   @Override
342:   public void updateLocation(String orderId, Location location) {
343:     throw new UnsupportedOperationException();
344:   }
345:   @Override
346:   public void noteDelivered(String orderId) {
347:     throw new UnsupportedOperationException();
348:   }
349:   @Override
350:   public Optional<Order> findOrder(String orderId) {
351:     Item item = table.getItem(new GetItemSpec()
352:             .withPrimaryKey(makePrimaryKey(orderId))
353:             .withConsistentRead(true));
354:     return Optional.ofNullable(item).map(this::toOrder);
355:   }
356:   private Order toOrder(Item avs) {
357:     Order order = new Order(avs.getString("orderId"),
358:             avs.getString("consumerId"),
359:             OrderState.valueOf(avs.getString("orderStatus")),
360:             toLineItems2(avs.getList("lineItems")),
361:             null,
362:             avs.getLong("restaurantId"),
363:             avs.getString("restaurantName"));
364:     if (avs.hasAttribute("creationDate"))
365:       order.setCreationDate(new DateTime(avs.getLong("creationDate")));
366:     return order;
367:   }
368:   private List<OrderLineItem> toLineItems2(List<LinkedHashMap<String,
369:           Object>> lineItems) {
370:     return lineItems.stream().map(this::toLineItem2).collect(toList());
371:   }
372:   private OrderLineItem toLineItem2(LinkedHashMap<String, Object>
373:                                             attributeValue) {
374:     return new OrderLineItem((String) attributeValue.get("menuItemId"),
375:                              (String) attributeValue.get("menuItemName"),
376:                              new Money((String) attributeValue.get("price")),
377:                             ((BigDecimal) attributeValue.get("quantity")).intValue()
378:             );
379:   }
380: }

34:           .build();
35:     } else {
36:       return AmazonDynamoDBClientBuilder
37:               .standard()
38:               .withRegion(awsRegion)
39:               .withCredentials(new AWSStaticCredentialsProvider(new BasicAWSCredentials(accessKey, secretKey)))
40:               .build();
41:     }
42:   }

File: ftgo-order-history-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/cqrs/orderhistory/dynamodb/SourceEvent.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.dynamodb;
 2: import com.amazonaws.services.dynamodbv2.document.spec.UpdateItemSpec;
 3: import java.util.HashMap;
 4: public class SourceEvent {
 5:   String aggregateType;
 6:   String aggregateId;
 7:   String eventId;
 8:   public SourceEvent(String aggregateType, String aggregateId, String eventId) {
 9:     this.aggregateType = aggregateType;
10:     this.aggregateId = aggregateId;
11:     this.eventId = eventId;
12:   }
13:   public String getAggregateType() {
14:     return aggregateType;
15:   }
16:   public UpdateItemSpec addDuplicateDetection(UpdateItemSpec spec) {
17:     HashMap<String, String> nameMap = spec.getNameMap() == null ? new HashMap<>() : new HashMap<>(spec.getNameMap());
18:     nameMap.put("#duplicateDetection", "events." + aggregateType + aggregateId);
19:     HashMap<String, Object> valueMap = new HashMap<>(spec.getValueMap());
20:     valueMap.put(":eventId", eventId);
21:     return spec.withUpdateExpression(String.format("%s , #duplicateDetection = :eventId", spec.getUpdateExpression()))
22:             .withNameMap(nameMap)
23:             .withValueMap(valueMap)
24:             .withConditionExpression(Expressions.and(spec.getConditionExpression(), "attribute_not_exists(#duplicateDetection) OR #duplicateDetection < :eventId"));
25:   }
26: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.messaging;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventEnvelope;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlers;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlersBuilder;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.DeliveryPickedUp;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.Location;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.OrderHistoryDao;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.dynamodb.Order;
 9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.dynamodb.SourceEvent;
10: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.*;
11: import org.slf4j.Logger;
12: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
13: import java.util.Optional;
14: public class OrderHistoryEventHandlers {
15:   private OrderHistoryDao orderHistoryDao;
16:   public OrderHistoryEventHandlers(OrderHistoryDao orderHistoryDao) {
17:     this.orderHistoryDao = orderHistoryDao;
18:   }
19:   private Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
20:   // TODO - determine events
21:   private String orderId;
22:   private Order order;
23:   private Location location; //
24:   public DomainEventHandlers domainEventHandlers() {
25:     return DomainEventHandlersBuilder
26:             .forAggregateType("net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.Order")
27:             .onEvent(OrderCreatedEvent.class, this::handleOrderCreated)
28:             .onEvent(OrderAuthorized.class, this::handleOrderAuthorized)
29:             .onEvent(OrderCancelled.class, this::handleOrderCancelled)
30:             .onEvent(OrderRejected.class, this::handleOrderRejected)
31: //            .onEvent(DeliveryPickedUp.class, this::handleDeliveryPickedUp)
32:             .build();
33:   }
34:   private Optional<SourceEvent> makeSourceEvent(DomainEventEnvelope<?> dee) {
35:     return Optional.of(new SourceEvent(dee.getAggregateType(),
36:             dee.getAggregateId(), dee.getEventId()));
37:   }
38:   public void handleOrderCreated(DomainEventEnvelope<OrderCreatedEvent> dee) {
39:     logger.debug("handleOrderCreated called {}", dee);
40:     boolean result = orderHistoryDao.addOrder(makeOrder(dee.getAggregateId(), dee.getEvent()), makeSourceEvent(dee));
41:     logger.debug("handleOrderCreated result {} {}", dee, result);
42:   }
43:   public void handleOrderAuthorized(DomainEventEnvelope<OrderAuthorized> dee) {
44:     logger.debug("handleOrderAuthorized called {}", dee);
45:     boolean result = orderHistoryDao.updateOrderState(dee.getAggregateId(), OrderState.APPROVED, makeSourceEvent(dee));
46:     logger.debug("handleOrderAuthorized result {} {}", dee, result);
47:   }
48:   public void handleOrderCancelled(DomainEventEnvelope<OrderCancelled> dee) {
49:     logger.debug("handleOrderCancelled called {}", dee);
50:     boolean result = orderHistoryDao.updateOrderState(dee.getAggregateId(), OrderState.CANCELLED, makeSourceEvent(dee));
51:     logger.debug("handleOrderCancelled result {} {}", dee, result);
52:   }
53:   public void handleOrderRejected(DomainEventEnvelope<OrderRejected> dee) {
54:     logger.debug("handleOrderRejected called {}", dee);
55:     boolean result = orderHistoryDao.updateOrderState(dee.getAggregateId(), OrderState.REJECTED, makeSourceEvent(dee));
56:     logger.debug("handleOrderRejected result {} {}", dee, result);
57:   }
58:   private Order makeOrder(String orderId, OrderCreatedEvent event) {
59:     return new Order(orderId,
60:             Long.toString(event.getOrderDetails().getConsumerId()),
61:             OrderState.APPROVAL_PENDING,
62:             event.getOrderDetails().getLineItems(),
63:             event.getOrderDetails().getOrderTotal(),
64:             event.getOrderDetails().getRestaurantId(),
65:             event.getRestaurantName());
66:   }
67:   public void handleDeliveryPickedUp(DomainEventEnvelope<DeliveryPickedUp>
68:                                              dee) {
69:     orderHistoryDao.notePickedUp(dee.getEvent().getOrderId(),
70:             makeSourceEvent(dee));
71:   }
72: /*
73:   // TODO - need a common API that abstracts message vs. event sourcing
74:   public void handleOrderCancelled() {
75:     orderHistoryDao.cancelOrder(orderId, null);
76:   }
77:   public void handleTicketPreparationStarted() {
78:     orderHistoryDao.noteTicketPreparationStarted(orderId);
79:   }
80:   public void handleTicketPreparationCompleted() {
81:     orderHistoryDao.noteTicketPreparationCompleted(orderId);
82:   }
83:   public void handleDeliveryLocationUpdated() {
84:     orderHistoryDao.updateLocation(orderId, location);
85:   }
86:   public void handleDeliveryDelivered() {
87:     orderHistoryDao.noteDelivered(orderId);
88:   }
89:   */
90: }

File: ftgo-order-history-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/cqrs/orderhistory/web/OrderHistoryController.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.web;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.OrderHistory;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.OrderHistoryDao;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.OrderHistoryFilter;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.cqrs.orderhistory.dynamodb.Order;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
 8: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
 9: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
10: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
11: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMethod;
12: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
13: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
14: import static java.util.stream.Collectors.toList;
15: @RestController
16: @RequestMapping(path = "/orders")
17: public class OrderHistoryController {
18:   private OrderHistoryDao orderHistoryDao;
19:   public OrderHistoryController(OrderHistoryDao orderHistoryDao) {
20:     this.orderHistoryDao = orderHistoryDao;
21:   }
22:   @RequestMapping(method = RequestMethod.GET)
23:   public ResponseEntity<GetOrdersResponse> getOrders(@RequestParam(name = "consumerId") String consumerId) {
24:     OrderHistory orderHistory = orderHistoryDao.findOrderHistory(consumerId, new OrderHistoryFilter());
25:     return new ResponseEntity<>(new GetOrdersResponse(orderHistory.getOrders()
26:             .stream()
27:             .map(this::makeGetOrderResponse).collect(toList()), orderHistory.getStartKey().orElse(null)), HttpStatus.OK);
28:   }
29:   private GetOrderResponse makeGetOrderResponse(Order order) {
30:     return new GetOrderResponse(order.getOrderId(), order.getStatus(), order.getRestaurantId(), order.getRestaurantName());
31:   }
32:   @RequestMapping(path = "/{orderId}", method = RequestMethod.GET)
33:   public ResponseEntity<GetOrderResponse> getOrder(@PathVariable String orderId) {
34:     return orderHistoryDao.findOrder(orderId)
35:             .map(order -> new ResponseEntity<>(makeGetOrderResponse(order), HttpStatus.OK))
36:             .orElseGet(() -> new ResponseEntity<>(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND));
37:   }
38: }

File: ftgo-order-service-api/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/api/events/OrderLineItem.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;
 3: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.EqualsBuilder;
 4: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.HashCodeBuilder;
 5: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.ToStringBuilder;
 6: import javax.persistence.AttributeOverride;
 7: import javax.persistence.AttributeOverrides;
 8: import javax.persistence.Column;
 9: import javax.persistence.Embeddable;
10: import javax.persistence.Embedded;
11: @Embeddable
12: public class OrderLineItem {
13:   public OrderLineItem() {
14:   }
15:   private int quantity;
16:   private String menuItemId;
17:   private String name;
18:   @Embedded
19:   @AttributeOverrides(@AttributeOverride(name="amount", column=@Column(name="price")))
20:   private Money price;
21:   @Override
22:   public String toString() {
23:     return ToStringBuilder.reflectionToString(this);
24:   }
25:   @Override
26:   public boolean equals(Object o) {
27:     return EqualsBuilder.reflectionEquals(this, o);
28:   }
29:   @Override
30:   public int hashCode() {
31:     return HashCodeBuilder.reflectionHashCode(this);
32:   }
33:   public OrderLineItem(String menuItemId, String name, Money price, int quantity) {
34:     this.menuItemId = menuItemId;
35:     this.name = name;
36:     this.price = price;
37:     this.quantity = quantity;
38:   }
39:   public Money deltaForChangedQuantity(int newQuantity) {
40:     return price.multiply(newQuantity - quantity);
41:   }
42:   public void setQuantity(int quantity) {
43:     this.quantity = quantity;
44:   }
45:   public void setMenuItemId(String menuItemId) {
46:     this.menuItemId = menuItemId;
47:   }
48:   public void setName(String name) {
49:     this.name = name;
50:   }
51:   public void setPrice(Money price) {
52:     this.price = price;
53:   }
54:   public int getQuantity() {
55:     return quantity;
56:   }
57:   public String getMenuItemId() {
58:     return menuItemId;
59:   }
60:   public String getName() {
61:     return name;
62:   }
63:   public Money getPrice() {
64:     return price;
65:   }
66:   public Money getTotal() {
67:     return price.multiply(quantity);
68:   }
69: }

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/domain/Order.java
================
  1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain;
  2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.aggregates.ResultWithDomainEvents;
  3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Address;
  4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;
  5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.UnsupportedStateTransitionException;
  6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.*;
  7: import javax.persistence.*;
  8: import java.util.List;
  9: import static net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderState.APPROVED;
 10: import static net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderState.APPROVAL_PENDING;
 11: import static net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderState.REJECTED;
 12: import static net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderState.REVISION_PENDING;
 13: import static java.util.Collections.emptyList;
 14: import static java.util.Collections.singletonList;
 15: @Entity
 16: @Table(name = "orders")
 17: @Access(AccessType.FIELD)
 18: public class Order {
 19:   public static ResultWithDomainEvents<Order, OrderDomainEvent>
 20:   createOrder(long consumerId, Restaurant restaurant, DeliveryInformation deliveryInformation, List<OrderLineItem> orderLineItems) {
 21:     Order order = new Order(consumerId, restaurant.getId(), deliveryInformation, orderLineItems);
 22:     List<OrderDomainEvent> events = singletonList(new OrderCreatedEvent(
 23:             new OrderDetails(consumerId, restaurant.getId(), orderLineItems,
 24:                     order.getOrderTotal()),
 25:             deliveryInformation.getDeliveryAddress(),
 26:             restaurant.getName()));
 27:     return new ResultWithDomainEvents<>(order, events);
 28:   }
 29:   @Id
 30:   @GeneratedValue
 31:   private Long id;
 32:   @Version
 33:   private Long version;
 34:   @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
 35:   private OrderState state;
 36:   private Long consumerId;
 37:   private Long restaurantId;
 38:   @Embedded
 39:   private OrderLineItems orderLineItems;
 40:   @Embedded
 41:   private DeliveryInformation deliveryInformation;
 42:   @Embedded
 43:   private PaymentInformation paymentInformation;
 44:   @Embedded
 45:   private Money orderMinimum = new Money(Integer.MAX_VALUE);
 46:   private Order() {
 47:   }
 48:   public Order(long consumerId, long restaurantId, DeliveryInformation deliveryInformation, List<OrderLineItem> orderLineItems) {
 49:     this.consumerId = consumerId;
 50:     this.restaurantId = restaurantId;
 51:     this.deliveryInformation = deliveryInformation;
 52:     this.orderLineItems = new OrderLineItems(orderLineItems);
 53:     this.state = APPROVAL_PENDING;
 54:   }
 55:   public Long getId() {
 56:     return id;
 57:   }
 58:   public void setId(Long id) {
 59:     this.id = id;
 60:   }
 61:   public DeliveryInformation getDeliveryInformation() {
 62:     return deliveryInformation;
 63:   }
 64:   public Money getOrderTotal() {
 65:     return orderLineItems.orderTotal();
 66:   }
 67:   public List<OrderDomainEvent> cancel() {
 68:     switch (state) {
 69:       case APPROVED:
 70:         this.state = OrderState.CANCEL_PENDING;
 71:         return emptyList();
 72:       default:
 73:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
 74:     }
 75:   }
 76:   public List<OrderDomainEvent> undoPendingCancel() {
 77:     switch (state) {
 78:       case CANCEL_PENDING:
 79:         this.state = OrderState.APPROVED;
 80:         return emptyList();
 81:       default:
 82:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
 83:     }
 84:   }
 85:   public List<OrderDomainEvent> noteCancelled() {
 86:     switch (state) {
 87:       case CANCEL_PENDING:
 88:         this.state = OrderState.CANCELLED;
 89:         return singletonList(new OrderCancelled());
 90:       default:
 91:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
 92:     }
 93:   }
 94:   public List<OrderDomainEvent> noteApproved() {
 95:     switch (state) {
 96:       case APPROVAL_PENDING:
 97:         this.state = APPROVED;
 98:         return singletonList(new OrderAuthorized());
 99:       default:
100:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
101:     }
102:   }
103:   public List<OrderDomainEvent> noteRejected() {
104:     switch (state) {
105:       case APPROVAL_PENDING:
106:         this.state = REJECTED;
107:         return singletonList(new OrderRejected());
108:       default:
109:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
110:     }
111:   }
112:   public List<OrderDomainEvent> noteReversingAuthorization() {
113:     return null;
114:   }
115:   public ResultWithDomainEvents<LineItemQuantityChange, OrderDomainEvent> revise(OrderRevision orderRevision) {
116:     switch (state) {
117:       case APPROVED:
118:         LineItemQuantityChange change = orderLineItems.lineItemQuantityChange(orderRevision);
119:         if (change.newOrderTotal.isGreaterThanOrEqual(orderMinimum)) {
120:           throw new OrderMinimumNotMetException();
121:         }
122:         this.state = REVISION_PENDING;
123:         return new ResultWithDomainEvents<>(change, singletonList(new OrderRevisionProposed(orderRevision, change.currentOrderTotal, change.newOrderTotal)));
124:       default:
125:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
126:     }
127:   }
128:   public List<OrderDomainEvent> rejectRevision() {
129:     switch (state) {
130:       case REVISION_PENDING:
131:         this.state = APPROVED;
132:         return emptyList();
133:       default:
134:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
135:     }
136:   }
137:   public List<OrderDomainEvent> confirmRevision(OrderRevision orderRevision) {
138:     switch (state) {
139:       case REVISION_PENDING:
140:         LineItemQuantityChange licd = orderLineItems.lineItemQuantityChange(orderRevision);
141:         orderRevision.getDeliveryInformation().ifPresent(newDi -> this.deliveryInformation = newDi);
142:         if (orderRevision.getRevisedOrderLineItems() != null && orderRevision.getRevisedOrderLineItems().size() > 0) {
143:           orderLineItems.updateLineItems(orderRevision);
144:         }
145:         this.state = APPROVED;
146:         return singletonList(new OrderRevised(orderRevision, licd.currentOrderTotal, licd.newOrderTotal));
147:       default:
148:         throw new UnsupportedStateTransitionException(state);
149:     }
150:   }
151:   public Long getVersion() {
152:     return version;
153:   }
154:   public List<OrderLineItem> getLineItems() {
155:     return orderLineItems.getLineItems();
156:   }
157:   public OrderState getState() {
158:     return state;
159:   }
160:   public long getRestaurantId() {
161:     return restaurantId;
162:   }
163:   public Long getConsumerId() {
164:     return consumerId;
165:   }
166: }

================
File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/domain/OrderLineItems.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderLineItem;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.RevisedOrderLineItem;
 5: import javax.persistence.CollectionTable;
 6: import javax.persistence.ElementCollection;
 7: import javax.persistence.Embeddable;
 8: import java.util.List;
 9: import java.util.Objects;
10: import java.util.Optional;
11: @Embeddable
12: public class OrderLineItems {
13:   @ElementCollection
14:   @CollectionTable(name = "order_line_items")
15:   private List<OrderLineItem> lineItems;
16:   private OrderLineItems() {
17:   }
18:   public OrderLineItems(List<OrderLineItem> lineItems) {
19:     this.lineItems = lineItems;
20:   }
21:   public List<OrderLineItem> getLineItems() {
22:     return lineItems;
23:   }
24:   public void setLineItems(List<OrderLineItem> lineItems) {
25:     this.lineItems = lineItems;
26:   }
27:   OrderLineItem findOrderLineItem(String lineItemId) {
28:     return lineItems.stream().filter(li -> li.getMenuItemId().equals(lineItemId)).findFirst().get();
29:   }
30:   Money changeToOrderTotal(OrderRevision orderRevision) {
31:     return orderRevision
32:             .getRevisedOrderLineItems()
33:             .stream()
34:             .map(item -> {
35:               OrderLineItem lineItem = findOrderLineItem(item.getMenuItemId());
36:               return lineItem.deltaForChangedQuantity(item.getQuantity());
37:             })
38:             .reduce(Money.ZERO, Money::add);
39:   }
40:   void updateLineItems(OrderRevision orderRevision) {
41:     getLineItems().stream().forEach(li -> {
42:       Optional<Integer> revised = orderRevision.getRevisedOrderLineItems()
43:               .stream()
44:               .filter(item -> Objects.equals(li.getMenuItemId(), item.getMenuItemId()))
45:               .map(RevisedOrderLineItem::getQuantity)
46:               .findFirst();
47:       li.setQuantity(revised.orElseThrow(() ->
48:               new IllegalArgumentException(String.format("menu item id not found.", li.getMenuItemId()))));
49:     });
50:   }
51:   Money orderTotal() {
52:     return lineItems.stream().map(OrderLineItem::getTotal).reduce(Money.ZERO, Money::add);
53:   }
54:   LineItemQuantityChange lineItemQuantityChange(OrderRevision orderRevision) {
55:     Money currentOrderTotal = orderTotal();
56:     Money delta = changeToOrderTotal(orderRevision);
57:     Money newOrderTotal = currentOrderTotal.add(delta);
58:     return new LineItemQuantityChange(currentOrderTotal, newOrderTotal, delta);
59:   }
60: }

================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.common.DomainEvent;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/domain/OrderService.java
================
  1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain;
  2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.aggregates.ResultWithDomainEvents;
  3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.publisher.DomainEventPublisher;
  4: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.orchestration.SagaInstanceFactory;
  5: import io.micrometer.core.instrument.MeterRegistry;
  6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderDetails;
  7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderDomainEvent;
  8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderLineItem;
  9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.cancelorder.CancelOrderSaga;
 10: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.cancelorder.CancelOrderSagaData;
 11: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.createorder.CreateOrderSaga;
 12: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.createorder.CreateOrderSagaState;
 13: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.reviseorder.ReviseOrderSaga;
 14: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.reviseorder.ReviseOrderSagaData;
 15: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.web.MenuItemIdAndQuantity;
 16: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 17: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 18: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation;
 19: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
 20: import java.util.List;
 21: import java.util.Optional;
 22: import java.util.function.Function;
 23: import static java.util.stream.Collectors.toList;
 24: public class OrderService {
 25:   private Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
 26:   private SagaInstanceFactory sagaInstanceFactory;
 27:   private OrderRepository orderRepository;
 28:   private RestaurantRepository restaurantRepository;
 29:   private CreateOrderSaga createOrderSaga;
 30:   private CancelOrderSaga cancelOrderSaga;
 31:   private ReviseOrderSaga reviseOrderSaga;
 32:   private OrderDomainEventPublisher orderAggregateEventPublisher;
 33:   private Optional<MeterRegistry> meterRegistry;
 34:   public OrderService(SagaInstanceFactory sagaInstanceFactory,
 35:                       OrderRepository orderRepository,
 36:                       DomainEventPublisher eventPublisher,
 37:                       RestaurantRepository restaurantRepository,
 38:                       CreateOrderSaga createOrderSaga,
 39:                       CancelOrderSaga cancelOrderSaga,
 40:                       ReviseOrderSaga reviseOrderSaga,
 41:                       OrderDomainEventPublisher orderAggregateEventPublisher,
 42:                       Optional<MeterRegistry> meterRegistry) {
 43:     this.sagaInstanceFactory = sagaInstanceFactory;
 44:     this.orderRepository = orderRepository;
 45:     this.restaurantRepository = restaurantRepository;
 46:     this.createOrderSaga = createOrderSaga;
 47:     this.cancelOrderSaga = cancelOrderSaga;
 48:     this.reviseOrderSaga = reviseOrderSaga;
 49:     this.orderAggregateEventPublisher = orderAggregateEventPublisher;
 50:     this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
 51:   }
 52:   @Transactional
 53:   public Order createOrder(long consumerId, long restaurantId, DeliveryInformation deliveryInformation,
 54:                            List<MenuItemIdAndQuantity> lineItems) {
 55:     Restaurant restaurant = restaurantRepository.findById(restaurantId)
 56:             .orElseThrow(() -> new RestaurantNotFoundException(restaurantId));
 57:     List<OrderLineItem> orderLineItems = makeOrderLineItems(lineItems, restaurant);
 58:     ResultWithDomainEvents<Order, OrderDomainEvent> orderAndEvents =
 59:             Order.createOrder(consumerId, restaurant, deliveryInformation, orderLineItems);
 60:     Order order = orderAndEvents.result;
 61:     orderRepository.save(order);
 62:     orderAggregateEventPublisher.publish(order, orderAndEvents.events);
 63:     OrderDetails orderDetails = new OrderDetails(consumerId, restaurantId, orderLineItems, order.getOrderTotal());
 64:     CreateOrderSagaState data = new CreateOrderSagaState(order.getId(), orderDetails);
 65:     sagaInstanceFactory.create(createOrderSaga, data);
 66:     meterRegistry.ifPresent(mr -> mr.counter("placed_orders").increment());
 67:     return order;
 68:   }
 69:   private List<OrderLineItem> makeOrderLineItems(List<MenuItemIdAndQuantity> lineItems, Restaurant restaurant) {
 70:     return lineItems.stream().map(li -> {
 71:       MenuItem om = restaurant.findMenuItem(li.getMenuItemId()).orElseThrow(() -> new InvalidMenuItemIdException(li.getMenuItemId()));
 72:       return new OrderLineItem(li.getMenuItemId(), om.getName(), om.getPrice(), li.getQuantity());
 73:     }).collect(toList());
 74:   }
 75:   public Optional<Order> confirmChangeLineItemQuantity(Long orderId, OrderRevision orderRevision) {
 76:     return orderRepository.findById(orderId).map(order -> {
 77:       List<OrderDomainEvent> events = order.confirmRevision(orderRevision);
 78:       orderAggregateEventPublisher.publish(order, events);
 79:       return order;
 80:     });
 81:   }
 82:   public void noteReversingAuthorization(Long orderId) {
 83:     throw new UnsupportedOperationException();
 84:   }
 85:   @Transactional
 86:   public Order cancel(Long orderId) {
 87:     Order order = orderRepository.findById(orderId)
 88:             .orElseThrow(() -> new OrderNotFoundException(orderId));
 89:     CancelOrderSagaData sagaData = new CancelOrderSagaData(order.getConsumerId(), orderId, order.getOrderTotal());
 90:     sagaInstanceFactory.create(cancelOrderSaga, sagaData);
 91:     return order;
 92:   }
 93:   private Order updateOrder(long orderId, Function<Order, List<OrderDomainEvent>> updater) {
 94:     return orderRepository.findById(orderId).map(order -> {
 95:       orderAggregateEventPublisher.publish(order, updater.apply(order));
 96:       return order;
 97:     }).orElseThrow(() -> new OrderNotFoundException(orderId));
 98:   }
 99:   public void approveOrder(long orderId) {
100:     updateOrder(orderId, Order::noteApproved);
101:     meterRegistry.ifPresent(mr -> mr.counter("approved_orders").increment());
102:   }
103:   public void rejectOrder(long orderId) {
104:     updateOrder(orderId, Order::noteRejected);
105:     meterRegistry.ifPresent(mr -> mr.counter("rejected_orders").increment());
106:   }
107:   public void beginCancel(long orderId) {
108:     updateOrder(orderId, Order::cancel);
109:   }
110:   public void undoCancel(long orderId) {
111:     updateOrder(orderId, Order::undoPendingCancel);
112:   }
113:   public void confirmCancelled(long orderId) {
114:     updateOrder(orderId, Order::noteCancelled);
115:   }
116:   @Transactional
117:   public Order reviseOrder(long orderId, OrderRevision orderRevision) {
118:     Order order = orderRepository.findById(orderId).orElseThrow(() -> new OrderNotFoundException(orderId));
119:     ReviseOrderSagaData sagaData = new ReviseOrderSagaData(order.getConsumerId(), orderId, null, orderRevision);
120:     sagaInstanceFactory.create(reviseOrderSaga, sagaData);
121:     return order;
122:   }
123:   public Optional<RevisedOrder> beginReviseOrder(long orderId, OrderRevision revision) {
124:     return orderRepository.findById(orderId).map(order -> {
125:       ResultWithDomainEvents<LineItemQuantityChange, OrderDomainEvent> result = order.revise(revision);
126:       orderAggregateEventPublisher.publish(order, result.events);
127:       return new RevisedOrder(order, result.result);
128:     });
129:   }
130:   public void undoPendingRevision(long orderId) {
131:     updateOrder(orderId, Order::rejectRevision);
132:   }
133:   public void confirmRevision(long orderId, OrderRevision revision) {
134:     updateOrder(orderId, order -> order.confirmRevision(revision));
135:   }
136:   public void createMenu(long id, String name, List<MenuItem> menuItems) {
137:     Restaurant restaurant = new Restaurant(id, name, menuItems);
138:     restaurantRepository.save(restaurant);
139:   }
140:   public void reviseMenu(long id, List<MenuItem> menuItems) {
141:     restaurantRepository.findById(id).map(restaurant -> {
142:       List<OrderDomainEvent> events = restaurant.reviseMenu(menuItems);
143:       return restaurant;
144:     }).orElseThrow(RuntimeException::new);
145:   }
146: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.common.DomainEvent;
 3: import org.springframework.util.Assert;
 4: import java.util.Arrays;
 5: import java.util.LinkedList;
 6: import java.util.List;
 7: public class Result {
 8:   private final List<DomainEvent> events;
 9:   private Boolean allowed;
10:   public List<DomainEvent> getEvents() {
11:     return events;
12:   }
13:   public Result(List<DomainEvent> events, Boolean allowed) {
14:     this.events = events;
15:     this.allowed = allowed;
16:   }
17:   public boolean isWhatIsThisCalled() {
18:     return allowed;
19:   }
20:   public static Builder build() {
21:     return new Builder();
22:   }
23:   public static class Builder {
24:     private List<DomainEvent> events = new LinkedList<>();
25:     private Boolean allowed;
26:     public Builder withEvents(DomainEvent... events) {
27:       Arrays.stream(events).forEach(e -> this.events.add(e));
28:       return this;
29:     }
30:     public Builder pending() {
31:       this.allowed = false;
32:       return this;
33:     }
34:     public Result build() {
35:       Assert.notNull(allowed);
36:       return new Result(events, allowed);
37:     }
38:     public Builder allowed() {
39:       this.allowed = false;
40:       return this;
41:     }
42:   }
43: }

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/grpc/OrderServiceServer.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.grpc;
 2: import io.grpc.Server;
 3: import io.grpc.ServerBuilder;
 4: import io.grpc.stub.StreamObserver;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Address;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.DeliveryInformation;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.Order;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.OrderService;
 9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.web.MenuItemIdAndQuantity;
10: import org.apache.commons.lang.StringUtils;
11: import org.slf4j.Logger;
12: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
13: import javax.annotation.PostConstruct;
14: import javax.annotation.PreDestroy;
15: import java.io.IOException;
16: import java.time.LocalDateTime;
17: import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
18: import java.util.List;
19: import static java.util.stream.Collectors.toList;
20: public class OrderServiceServer {
21:   private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OrderServiceServer.class);
22:   private int port = 50051;
23:   private Server server;
24:   private OrderService orderService;
25:   public OrderServiceServer(OrderService orderService) {
26:     this.orderService = orderService;
27:   }
28:   @PostConstruct
29:   public void start() throws IOException {
30:     server = ServerBuilder.forPort(port)
31:             .addService(new OrderServiceImpl())
32:             .build()
33:             .start();
34:     logger.info("Server started, listening on " + port);
35:   }
36:   @PreDestroy
37:   public void stop() {
38:     if (server != null) {
39:       logger.info("*** shutting down gRPC server since JVM is shutting down");
40:       server.shutdown();
41:       logger.info("*** server shut down");
42:     }
43:   }
44:   private class OrderServiceImpl extends OrderServiceGrpc.OrderServiceImplBase {
45:     @Override
46:     public void createOrder(CreateOrderRequest req, StreamObserver<CreateOrderReply> responseObserver) {
47:       List<LineItem> lineItemsList = req.getLineItemsList();
48:       Order order = orderService.createOrder(req.getConsumerId(),
49:               req.getRestaurantId(),
50:               new DeliveryInformation(LocalDateTime.parse(req.getDeliveryTime(), DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME), makeAddress(req.getDeliveryAddress())),
51:               lineItemsList.stream().map(x -> new MenuItemIdAndQuantity(x.getMenuItemId(), x.getQuantity())).collect(toList())
52:       );
53:       CreateOrderReply reply = CreateOrderReply.newBuilder().setOrderId(order.getId()).build();
54:       responseObserver.onNext(reply);
55:       responseObserver.onCompleted();
56:     }
57:     private Address makeAddress(net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.grpc.Address address) {
58:       return new Address(address.getStreet1(), nullIfBlank(address.getStreet2()), address.getCity(), address.getState(), address.getZip());
59:     }
60:     @Override
61:     public void cancelOrder(CancelOrderRequest req, StreamObserver<CancelOrderReply> responseObserver) {
62:       CancelOrderReply reply = CancelOrderReply.newBuilder().setMessage("Hello " + req.getName()).build();
63:       responseObserver.onNext(reply);
64:       responseObserver.onCompleted();
65:     }
66:     @Override
67:     public void reviseOrder(ReviseOrderRequest req, StreamObserver<ReviseOrderReply> responseObserver) {
68:       ReviseOrderReply reply = ReviseOrderReply.newBuilder().setMessage("Hello " + req.getName()).build();
69:       responseObserver.onNext(reply);
70:       responseObserver.onCompleted();
71:     }
72:   }
73:   private String nullIfBlank(String s) {
74:     return StringUtils.isBlank(s) ? null : s;
75:   }

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/messaging/OrderEventConsumer.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.messaging;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventEnvelope;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlers;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventHandlersBuilder;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.OrderService;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.RestaurantCreated;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.RestaurantMenuRevised;
 8: public class OrderEventConsumer {
 9:   private OrderService orderService;
10:   public OrderEventConsumer(OrderService orderService) {
11:     this.orderService = orderService;
12:   }
13:   public DomainEventHandlers domainEventHandlers() {
14:     return DomainEventHandlersBuilder
15:             .forAggregateType("net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.Restaurant")
16:             .onEvent(RestaurantCreated.class, this::createMenu)
17:             .onEvent(RestaurantMenuRevised.class, this::reviseMenu)
18:             .build();
19:   }
20:   private void createMenu(DomainEventEnvelope<RestaurantCreated> de) {
21:     String restaurantIds = de.getAggregateId();
22:     long id = Long.parseLong(restaurantIds);
23:     orderService.createMenu(id, de.getEvent().getName(), RestaurantEventMapper.toMenuItems(de.getEvent().getMenu().getMenuItems()));
24:   }
25:   public void reviseMenu(DomainEventEnvelope<RestaurantMenuRevised> de) {
26:     String restaurantIds = de.getAggregateId();
27:     long id = Long.parseLong(restaurantIds);
28:     orderService.reviseMenu(id, RestaurantEventMapper.toMenuItems(de.getEvent().getMenu().getMenuItems()));
29:   }
30: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.messaging;

 3: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventDispatcher;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.events.subscriber.DomainEventDispatcherFactory;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.OrderService;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.OrderServiceWithRepositoriesConfiguration;
 7: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 8: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 9: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
10: @Configuration
11: @Import({OrderServiceWithRepositoriesConfiguration.class, TramEventSubscriberConfiguration.class})
12: public class OrderServiceMessagingConfiguration {

18:   public DomainEventDispatcher domainEventDispatcher(OrderEventConsumer orderEventConsumer, DomainEventDispatcherFactory domainEventDispatcherFactory) {

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/messaging/RestaurantEventMapper.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.messaging;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Money;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.Address;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.MenuItem;
 5: import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
 6: import java.util.List;
 7: import java.util.stream.Collectors;
 8: public class RestaurantEventMapper {
 9:   @NotNull
10:   public static List<MenuItem> fromMenuItems(List<net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.MenuItem> menuItems) {
11:     return menuItems.stream().map(mi -> new MenuItem().withId(mi.getId()).withName(mi.getName()).withPrice(mi.getPrice().asString())).collect(Collectors.toList());
12:   }
13:   public static Address fromAddress(net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.Address a) {
14:     return new Address().withStreet1(a.getStreet1()).withStreet2(a.getStreet2()).withCity(a.getCity()).withZip(a.getZip());
15:   }
16:   public static List<net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.MenuItem> toMenuItems(List<MenuItem> menuItems) {
17:     return menuItems.stream().map(mi -> new net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.MenuItem(mi.getId(), mi.getName(), new Money(mi.getPrice()))).collect(Collectors.toList());
18:   }
19: }

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/sagas/cancelorder/CancelOrderSaga.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.cancelorder;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandWithDestination;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.orchestration.SagaDefinition;
 4: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.simpledsl.SimpleSaga;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountservice.api.AccountingServiceChannels;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountservice.api.ReverseAuthorizationCommand;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.OrderServiceChannels;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.BeginCancelCommand;
 9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.ConfirmCancelOrderCommand;
10: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.UndoBeginCancelCommand;
11: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.BeginCancelTicketCommand;
12: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.ConfirmCancelTicketCommand;
13: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.KitchenServiceChannels;
14: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.UndoBeginCancelTicketCommand;
15: import org.springframework.util.Assert;
16: import javax.annotation.PostConstruct;
17: import static io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandWithDestinationBuilder.send;
18: public class CancelOrderSaga implements SimpleSaga<CancelOrderSagaData> {
19:   private SagaDefinition<CancelOrderSagaData> sagaDefinition;
20:   @PostConstruct
21:   public void initializeSagaDefinition() {
22:     sagaDefinition = step()
23:             .invokeParticipant(this::beginCancel)
24:             .withCompensation(this::undoBeginCancel)
25:             .step()
26:             .invokeParticipant(this::beginCancelTicket)
27:             .withCompensation(this::undoBeginCancelTicket)
28:             .step()
29:             .invokeParticipant(this::reverseAuthorization)
30:             .step()
31:             .invokeParticipant(this::confirmTicketCancel)
32:             .step()
33:             .invokeParticipant(this::confirmOrderCancel)
34:             .build();
35:   }
36:   private CommandWithDestination confirmOrderCancel(CancelOrderSagaData data) {
37:     return send(new ConfirmCancelOrderCommand(data.getOrderId()))
38:             .to(OrderServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
39:             .build();
40:   }
41:   private CommandWithDestination confirmTicketCancel(CancelOrderSagaData data) {
42:     return send(new ConfirmCancelTicketCommand(data.getRestaurantId(), data.getOrderId()))
43:             .to(KitchenServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
44:             .build();
45:   }
46:   private CommandWithDestination reverseAuthorization(CancelOrderSagaData data) {
47:     return send(new ReverseAuthorizationCommand(data.getConsumerId(), data.getOrderId(), data.getOrderTotal()))
48:             .to(AccountingServiceChannels.accountingServiceChannel)
49:             .build();
50:   }
51:   private CommandWithDestination undoBeginCancelTicket(CancelOrderSagaData data) {
52:     return send(new UndoBeginCancelTicketCommand(data.getRestaurantId(), data.getOrderId()))
53:             .to(KitchenServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
54:             .build();
55:   }
56:   private CommandWithDestination beginCancelTicket(CancelOrderSagaData data) {
57:     return send(new BeginCancelTicketCommand(data.getRestaurantId(), (long) data.getOrderId()))
58:             .to(KitchenServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
59:             .build();
60:   }
61:   private CommandWithDestination undoBeginCancel(CancelOrderSagaData data) {
62:     return send(new UndoBeginCancelCommand(data.getOrderId()))
63:             .to(OrderServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
64:             .build();
65:   }
66:   private CommandWithDestination beginCancel(CancelOrderSagaData data) {
67:     return send(new BeginCancelCommand(data.getOrderId()))
68:             .to(OrderServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
69:             .build();
70:   }
71:   @Override
72:   public SagaDefinition<CancelOrderSagaData> getSagaDefinition() {
73:     Assert.notNull(sagaDefinition);
74:     return sagaDefinition;
75:   }
76: }

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/sagas/createorder/CreateOrderSaga.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.createorder;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.orchestration.SagaDefinition;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.simpledsl.SimpleSaga;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.*;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.CreateTicketReply;
 6: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 7: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 8: public class CreateOrderSaga implements SimpleSaga<CreateOrderSagaState> {
 9:   private Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
10:   private SagaDefinition<CreateOrderSagaState> sagaDefinition;
11:   public CreateOrderSaga(OrderServiceProxy orderService, ConsumerServiceProxy consumerService, KitchenServiceProxy kitchenService,
12:                          AccountingServiceProxy accountingService) {
13:     this.sagaDefinition =
14:              step()
15:               .withCompensation(orderService.reject, CreateOrderSagaState::makeRejectOrderCommand)
16:             .step()
17:               .invokeParticipant(consumerService.validateOrder, CreateOrderSagaState::makeValidateOrderByConsumerCommand)
18:             .step()
19:               .invokeParticipant(kitchenService.create, CreateOrderSagaState::makeCreateTicketCommand)
20:               .onReply(CreateTicketReply.class, CreateOrderSagaState::handleCreateTicketReply)
21:               .withCompensation(kitchenService.cancel, CreateOrderSagaState::makeCancelCreateTicketCommand)
22:             .step()
23:                 .invokeParticipant(accountingService.authorize, CreateOrderSagaState::makeAuthorizeCommand)
24:             .step()
25:               .invokeParticipant(kitchenService.confirmCreate, CreateOrderSagaState::makeConfirmCreateTicketCommand)
26:             .step()
27:               .invokeParticipant(orderService.approve, CreateOrderSagaState::makeApproveOrderCommand)
28:             .build();
29:   }
30:   @Override
31:   public SagaDefinition<CreateOrderSagaState> getSagaDefinition() {
32:     return sagaDefinition;
33:   }
34: }

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/sagas/createorder/CreateOrderSagaState.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.createorder;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountservice.api.AuthorizeCommand;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.consumerservice.api.ValidateOrderByConsumer;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderDetails;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.events.OrderLineItem;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.ApproveOrderCommand;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.RejectOrderCommand;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.*;
 9: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.EqualsBuilder;
10: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.HashCodeBuilder;
11: import org.slf4j.Logger;
12: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
13: import java.util.List;
14: import static java.util.stream.Collectors.toList;
15: public class CreateOrderSagaState {

17:   private Long orderId;

22:   }

28:   }

31:     return EqualsBuilder.reflectionEquals(this, o);
32:   }

35:     return HashCodeBuilder.reflectionHashCode(this);
36:   }
37:   public OrderDetails getOrderDetails() {
38:     return orderDetails;
39:   }

71:   }

73:     ValidateOrderByConsumer x = new ValidateOrderByConsumer();
74:     x.setConsumerId(getOrderDetails().getConsumerId());

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/sagas/reviseorder/ReviseOrderSaga.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagas.reviseorder;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandWithDestination;
 3: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.simpledsl.SimpleSaga;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountservice.api.AccountingServiceChannels;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.OrderServiceChannels;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.BeginReviseOrderCommand;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.BeginReviseOrderReply;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.BeginReviseTicketCommand;
 9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.ConfirmReviseOrderCommand;
10: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.accountservice.api.ReviseAuthorization;
11: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.UndoBeginReviseOrderCommand;
12: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.ConfirmReviseTicketCommand;
13: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.KitchenServiceChannels;
14: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.kitchenservice.api.UndoBeginReviseTicketCommand;
15: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.orchestration.SagaDefinition;
16: import org.slf4j.Logger;
17: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
18: import javax.annotation.PostConstruct;
19: import static io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandWithDestinationBuilder.send;
20: public class ReviseOrderSaga implements SimpleSaga<ReviseOrderSagaData> {
21:   private Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
22:   private SagaDefinition<ReviseOrderSagaData> sagaDefinition;
23:   @PostConstruct
24:   public void initializeSagaDefinition() {
25:     sagaDefinition = step()
26:             .invokeParticipant(this::beginReviseOrder)
27:             .onReply(BeginReviseOrderReply.class, this::handleBeginReviseOrderReply)
28:             .withCompensation(this::undoBeginReviseOrder)
29:             .step()
30:             .invokeParticipant(this::beginReviseTicket)
31:             .withCompensation(this::undoBeginReviseTicket)
32:             .step()
33:             .invokeParticipant(this::reviseAuthorization)
34:             .step()
35:             .invokeParticipant(this::confirmTicketRevision)
36:             .step()
37:             .invokeParticipant(this::confirmOrderRevision)
38:             .build();
39:   }
40:   private void handleBeginReviseOrderReply(ReviseOrderSagaData data, BeginReviseOrderReply reply) {
41:     logger.info("ƒ order total: {}", reply.getRevisedOrderTotal());
42:     data.setRevisedOrderTotal(reply.getRevisedOrderTotal());
43:   }
44:   @Override
45:   public SagaDefinition<ReviseOrderSagaData> getSagaDefinition() {
46:     return sagaDefinition;
47:   }
48:   private CommandWithDestination confirmOrderRevision(ReviseOrderSagaData data) {
49:     return send(new ConfirmReviseOrderCommand(data.getOrderId(), data.getOrderRevision()))
50:             .to(OrderServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
51:             .build();
52:   }
53:   private CommandWithDestination confirmTicketRevision(ReviseOrderSagaData data) {
54:     return send(new ConfirmReviseTicketCommand(data.getRestaurantId(), data.getOrderId(), data.getOrderRevision().getRevisedOrderLineItems()))
55:             .to(KitchenServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
56:             .build();
57:   }
58:   private CommandWithDestination reviseAuthorization(ReviseOrderSagaData data) {
59:     return send(new ReviseAuthorization(data.getConsumerId(), data.getOrderId(), data.getRevisedOrderTotal()))
60:             .to(AccountingServiceChannels.accountingServiceChannel)
61:             .build();
62:   }
63:   private CommandWithDestination undoBeginReviseTicket(ReviseOrderSagaData data) {
64:     return send(new UndoBeginReviseTicketCommand(data.getRestaurantId(), data.getOrderId()))
65:             .to(KitchenServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
66:             .build();
67:   }
68:   private CommandWithDestination beginReviseTicket(ReviseOrderSagaData data) {
69:     return send(new BeginReviseTicketCommand(data.getRestaurantId(), data.getOrderId(), data.getOrderRevision().getRevisedOrderLineItems()))
70:             .to(KitchenServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
71:             .build();
72:   }
73:   private CommandWithDestination undoBeginReviseOrder(ReviseOrderSagaData data) {
74:     return send(new UndoBeginReviseOrderCommand(data.getOrderId()))
75:             .to(OrderServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
76:             .build();
77:   }
78:   private CommandWithDestination beginReviseOrder(ReviseOrderSagaData data) {
79:     return send(new BeginReviseOrderCommand(data.getOrderId(), data.getOrderRevision()))
80:             .to(OrderServiceChannels.COMMAND_CHANNEL)
81:             .build();
82:   }
83: }

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/service/OrderCommandHandlers.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.service;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlerReplyBuilder;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.OrderRepository;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.OrderRevision;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.OrderService;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.RevisedOrder;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.sagaparticipants.*;
 8: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlers;
 9: import io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandMessage;
10: import io.eventuate.tram.events.publisher.DomainEventPublisher;
11: import io.eventuate.tram.messaging.common.Message;
12: import io.eventuate.tram.sagas.participant.SagaCommandHandlersBuilder;
13: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.common.UnsupportedStateTransitionException;
14: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
15: import java.util.Optional;
16: import static io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlerReplyBuilder.withFailure;
17: import static io.eventuate.tram.commands.consumer.CommandHandlerReplyBuilder.withSuccess;
18: public class OrderCommandHandlers {
19:   @Autowired
20:   private OrderService orderService;
21:   public CommandHandlers commandHandlers() {
22:     return SagaCommandHandlersBuilder
23:           .fromChannel("orderService")
24:           .onMessage(ApproveOrderCommand.class, this::approveOrder)
25:           .onMessage(RejectOrderCommand.class, this::rejectOrder)
26:           .onMessage(BeginCancelCommand.class, this::beginCancel)
27:           .onMessage(UndoBeginCancelCommand.class, this::undoCancel)
28:           .onMessage(ConfirmCancelOrderCommand.class, this::confirmCancel)
29:           .onMessage(BeginReviseOrderCommand.class, this::beginReviseOrder)
30:           .onMessage(UndoBeginReviseOrderCommand.class, this::undoPendingRevision)
31:           .onMessage(ConfirmReviseOrderCommand.class, this::confirmRevision)
32:           .build();
33:   }
34:   public Message approveOrder(CommandMessage<ApproveOrderCommand> cm) {
35:     long orderId = cm.getCommand().getOrderId();
36:     orderService.approveOrder(orderId);
37:     return withSuccess();
38:   }
39:   public Message rejectOrder(CommandMessage<RejectOrderCommand> cm) {
40:     long orderId = cm.getCommand().getOrderId();
41:     orderService.rejectOrder(orderId);
42:     return withSuccess();
43:   }
44:   public Message beginCancel(CommandMessage<BeginCancelCommand> cm) {
45:     long orderId = cm.getCommand().getOrderId();
46:     try {
47:       orderService.beginCancel(orderId);
48:       return withSuccess();
49:     } catch (UnsupportedStateTransitionException e) {
50:       return withFailure();
51:     }
52:   }
53:   public Message undoCancel(CommandMessage<UndoBeginCancelCommand> cm) {
54:     long orderId = cm.getCommand().getOrderId();
55:     orderService.undoCancel(orderId);
56:     return withSuccess();
57:   }
58:   public Message confirmCancel(CommandMessage<ConfirmCancelOrderCommand> cm) {
59:     long orderId = cm.getCommand().getOrderId();
60:     orderService.confirmCancelled(orderId);
61:     return withSuccess();
62:   }
63:   public Message beginReviseOrder(CommandMessage<BeginReviseOrderCommand> cm) {
64:     long orderId = cm.getCommand().getOrderId();
65:     OrderRevision revision = cm.getCommand().getRevision();
66:     try {
67:       return orderService.beginReviseOrder(orderId, revision).map(result -> withSuccess(new BeginReviseOrderReply(result.getChange().getNewOrderTotal()))).orElseGet(CommandHandlerReplyBuilder::withFailure);
68:     } catch (UnsupportedStateTransitionException e) {
69:       return withFailure();
70:     }
71:   }
72:   public Message undoPendingRevision(CommandMessage <UndoBeginReviseOrderCommand> cm) {
73:     long orderId = cm.getCommand().getOrderId();
74:     orderService.undoPendingRevision(orderId);
75:     return withSuccess();
76:   }
77:   public Message confirmRevision(CommandMessage<ConfirmReviseOrderCommand> cm) {
78:     long orderId = cm.getCommand().getOrderId();
79:     OrderRevision revision = cm.getCommand().getRevision();
80:     orderService.confirmRevision(orderId, revision);
81:     return withSuccess();
82:   }
83: }

File: ftgo-order-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/orderservice/web/OrderController.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.web;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.web.CreateOrderRequest;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.web.CreateOrderResponse;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.api.web.ReviseOrderRequest;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.domain.*;
 6: import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
 7: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
 8: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
 9: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
10: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
11: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMethod;
12: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
13: import java.util.Optional;
14: import static java.util.stream.Collectors.toList;
15: @RestController
16: @RequestMapping(path = "/orders")
17: public class OrderController {
18:   private OrderService orderService;
19:   private OrderRepository orderRepository;
20:   public OrderController(OrderService orderService, OrderRepository orderRepository) {
21:     this.orderService = orderService;
22:     this.orderRepository = orderRepository;
23:   }
24:   @RequestMapping(method = RequestMethod.POST)
25:   public CreateOrderResponse create(@RequestBody CreateOrderRequest request) {
26:     Order order = orderService.createOrder(request.getConsumerId(),
27:             request.getRestaurantId(),
28:             new DeliveryInformation(request.getDeliveryTime(), request.getDeliveryAddress()),
29:             request.getLineItems().stream().map(x -> new MenuItemIdAndQuantity(x.getMenuItemId(), x.getQuantity())).collect(toList())
30:     );
31:     return new CreateOrderResponse(order.getId());
32:   }
33:   @RequestMapping(path = "/{orderId}", method = RequestMethod.GET)
34:   public ResponseEntity<GetOrderResponse> getOrder(@PathVariable long orderId) {
35:     Optional<Order> order = orderRepository.findById(orderId);
36:     return order.map(o -> new ResponseEntity<>(makeGetOrderResponse(o), HttpStatus.OK)).orElseGet(() -> new ResponseEntity<>(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND));
37:   }
38:   private GetOrderResponse makeGetOrderResponse(Order order) {
39:     return new GetOrderResponse(order.getId(), order.getState(), order.getOrderTotal());
40:   }
41:   @RequestMapping(path = "/{orderId}/cancel", method = RequestMethod.POST)
42:   public ResponseEntity<GetOrderResponse> cancel(@PathVariable long orderId) {
43:     try {
44:       Order order = orderService.cancel(orderId);
45:       return new ResponseEntity<>(makeGetOrderResponse(order), HttpStatus.OK);
46:     } catch (OrderNotFoundException e) {
47:       return new ResponseEntity<>(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);
48:     }
49:   }
50:   @RequestMapping(path = "/{orderId}/revise", method = RequestMethod.POST)
51:   public ResponseEntity<GetOrderResponse> revise(@PathVariable long orderId, @RequestBody ReviseOrderRequest request) {
52:     try {
53:       Order order = orderService.reviseOrder(orderId, new OrderRevision(Optional.empty(), request.getRevisedOrderLineItems()));
54:       return new ResponseEntity<>(makeGetOrderResponse(order), HttpStatus.OK);
55:     } catch (OrderNotFoundException e) {
56:       return new ResponseEntity<>(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);
57:     }
58:   }
59: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.orderservice.web;
 2: import brave.Span;
 3: import brave.Tracer;
 4: import org.springframework.cloud.sleuth.instrument.web.TraceWebServletAutoConfiguration;
 5: import org.springframework.core.annotation.Order;
 6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 7: import org.springframework.web.filter.GenericFilterBean;
 8: import javax.servlet.FilterChain;
 9: import javax.servlet.ServletException;
10: import javax.servlet.ServletRequest;
11: import javax.servlet.ServletResponse;
12: import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
13: import java.io.IOException;
14: @Component
15: @Order(TraceWebServletAutoConfiguration.TRACING_FILTER_ORDER + 1)
16: class TraceIdResponseFilter extends GenericFilterBean {
17:   private final Tracer tracer;
18:   public TraceIdResponseFilter(Tracer tracer) {
19:     this.tracer = tracer;
20:   }
21:   @Override public void doFilter(ServletRequest request, ServletResponse response,
22:                                  FilterChain chain) throws IOException, ServletException {
23:     Span currentSpan = this.tracer.currentSpan();
24:     if (currentSpan != null) {
25:       ((HttpServletResponse) response)
26:               .addHeader("ZIPKIN-TRACE-ID",
27:                       currentSpan.context().traceIdString());
28:     }
29:     chain.doFilter(request, response);
30:   }
31: }
