File: ftgo-restaurant-service-aws-lambda/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/restaurantservice/aws/AbstractHttpHandler.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws;
 2: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.Context;
 3: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.RequestHandler;
 4: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.events.APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent;
 5: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.events.APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent;
 6: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 7: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 8: import static net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws.ApiGatewayResponse.buildErrorResponse;
 9: public abstract class AbstractHttpHandler
10:         implements RequestHandler<APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent, APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent> {
11:   private Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(this.getClass());
12:   @Override
13:   public APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent handleRequest(APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent input, Context context) {
14:     log.debug("Got request: {}", input);
15:     try {
16:       beforeHandling(input, context);
17:       return handleHttpRequest(input, context);
18:     } catch (Exception e) {
19:       log.error("Error handling request id: {}", context.getAwsRequestId(), e);
20:       return buildErrorResponse(new AwsLambdaError(
21:               "Internal Server Error",
22:               "500",
23:               context.getAwsRequestId(),
24:               "Error handling request: " + context.getAwsRequestId() + " " + input.toString()));
25:     }
26:   }
27:   protected void beforeHandling(APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent request, Context context) {
28:     // do nothing
29:   }
30:   protected abstract APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent handleHttpRequest(APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent request, Context context);
31: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws;
 2: import org.apache.commons.lang.builder.ToStringBuilder;
 3: import java.util.Map;
 4: import java.util.Optional;
 5: public class ApiGatewayRequest {
 6:   private String resource;
 7:   private String path;
 8:   private String httpMethod;
 9:   private Map<String, String> headers;
10:   private Map<String, String> queryStringParameters;
11:   private Map<String, String> pathParameters;
12:   private Map<String, String> stageVariables;
13:   private RequestContext requestContext;
14:   private String body;
15:   private boolean isBase64Encoded;
16:   public String getResource() {
17:     return resource;
18:   }
19:   public void setResource(String resource) {
20:     this.resource = resource;
21:   }
22:   public String getPath() {
23:     return path;
24:   }
25:   public void setPath(String path) {
26:     this.path = path;
27:   }
28:   public String getHttpMethod() {
29:     return httpMethod;
30:   }
31:   public void setHttpMethod(String httpMethod) {
32:     this.httpMethod = httpMethod;
33:   }
34:   public Map<String, String> getHeaders() {
35:     return headers;
36:   }
37:   public void setHeaders(Map<String, String> headers) {
38:     this.headers = headers;
39:   }
40:   public Map<String, String> getQueryStringParameters() {
41:     return queryStringParameters;
42:   }
43:   public void setQueryStringParameters(Map<String, String> queryStringParameters) {
44:     this.queryStringParameters = queryStringParameters;
45:   }
46:   public Map<String, String> getPathParameters() {
47:     return pathParameters;
48:   }
49:   public void setPathParameters(Map<String, String> pathParameters) {
50:     this.pathParameters = pathParameters;
51:   }
52:   public Map<String, String> getStageVariables() {
53:     return stageVariables;
54:   }
55:   public void setStageVariables(Map<String, String> stageVariables) {
56:     this.stageVariables = stageVariables;
57:   }
58:   public RequestContext getRequestContext() {
59:     return requestContext;
60:   }
61:   public void setRequestContext(RequestContext requestContext) {
62:     this.requestContext = requestContext;
63:   }
64:   public String getBody() {
65:     return body;
66:   }
67:   public void setBody(String body) {
68:     this.body = body;
69:   }
70:   public boolean isBase64Encoded() {
71:     return isBase64Encoded;
72:   }
73:   public void setBase64Encoded(boolean base64Encoded) {
74:     isBase64Encoded = base64Encoded;
75:   }
76:   @Override
77:   public String toString() {
78:     return ToStringBuilder.reflectionToString(this);
79:   }
80:   public String getPathParam(String paramName) {
81:     return Optional.ofNullable(this.getPathParameters())
82:             .map(paramsMap -> paramsMap.get(paramName))
83:             .orElse(null);
84:   }
85: }

================
File: ftgo-restaurant-service-aws-lambda/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/restaurantservice/aws/ApiGatewayResponse.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws;
 2: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.events.APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent;
 3: import io.eventuate.common.json.mapper.JSonMapper;
 4: import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
 5: import java.util.Base64;
 6: import java.util.Collections;
 7: import java.util.HashMap;
 8: import java.util.Map;
 9: public class ApiGatewayResponse {
10:   private final int statusCode;
11:   private final String body;
12:   private final Map<String, String> headers;
13:   private final boolean isBase64Encoded;
14:   public ApiGatewayResponse(int statusCode, String body, Map<String, String> headers, boolean isBase64Encoded) {
15:     this.statusCode = statusCode;
16:     this.body = body;
17:     this.headers = headers;
18:     this.isBase64Encoded = isBase64Encoded;
19:   }
20:   public int getStatusCode() {
21:     return statusCode;
22:   }
23:   public String getBody() {
24:     return body;
25:   }
26:   public Map<String, String> getHeaders() {
27:     return headers;
28:   }
29:   // API Gateway expects the property to be called "isBase64Encoded" => isIs
30:   public boolean isIsBase64Encoded() {
31:     return isBase64Encoded;
32:   }
33:   public static Builder builder() {
34:     return new Builder();
35:   }
36:   public static class Builder {
37:     private int statusCode = 200;
38:     private Map<String, String> headers = Collections.emptyMap();
39:     private String rawBody;
40:     private Object objectBody;
41:     private byte[] binaryBody;
42:     private boolean base64Encoded;
43:     public Builder setStatusCode(int statusCode) {
44:       this.statusCode = statusCode;
45:       return this;
46:     }
47:     public Builder setHeaders(Map<String, String> headers) {
48:       this.headers = headers;
49:       return this;
50:     }
51:     public Builder setRawBody(String rawBody) {
52:       this.rawBody = rawBody;
53:       return this;
54:     }
55:     public Builder setObjectBody(Object objectBody) {
56:       this.objectBody = objectBody;
57:       return this;
58:     }
59:     public Builder setBinaryBody(byte[] binaryBody) {
60:       this.binaryBody = binaryBody;
61:       setBase64Encoded(true);
62:       return this;
63:     }
64:     public Builder setBase64Encoded(boolean base64Encoded) {
65:       this.base64Encoded = base64Encoded;
66:       return this;
67:     }
68:     public APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent build() {
69:       String body = null;
70:       if (rawBody != null) {
71:         body = rawBody;
72:       } else if (objectBody != null) {
73:         body = JSonMapper.toJson(objectBody);
74:       } else if (binaryBody != null) {
75:         body = new String(Base64.getEncoder().encode(binaryBody), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
76:       }
77:       APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent response = new APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent();
78:       response.setStatusCode(statusCode);
79:       response.setBody(body);
80:       response.setHeaders(headers);
81:       return response;
82:     }
83:   }
84:   public static APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent buildErrorResponse(AwsLambdaError error) {
85:     return ApiGatewayResponse.builder()
86:             .setStatusCode(Integer.valueOf(error.getCode()))
87:             .setObjectBody(error)
88:             .setHeaders(applicationJsonHeaders())
89:             .build();
90:   }
91:   public static Map<String, String> applicationJsonHeaders() {
92:     Map<String, String> headers = new HashMap<>();
93:     headers.put("Content-Type", "application/json");
94:     return headers;
95:   }
96: }

File: ftgo-restaurant-service-aws-lambda/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/restaurantservice/domain/RestaurantService.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain;
 2: import io.eventuate.tram.events.publisher.DomainEventPublisher;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.RestaurantCreated;
 4: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 5: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
 6: import java.util.Collections;
 7: import java.util.Optional;
 8: @Transactional
 9: public class RestaurantService {
10:   private RestaurantRepository restaurantRepository;
11:   @Autowired
12:   private DomainEventPublisher domainEventPublisher;
13:   public RestaurantService() {
14:   }
15:   public RestaurantService(RestaurantRepository restaurantRepository) {
16:     this.restaurantRepository = restaurantRepository;
17:   }
18:   public Restaurant create(CreateRestaurantRequest request) {
19:     Restaurant restaurant = new Restaurant(request.getName(), request.getMenu());
20:     restaurantRepository.save(restaurant);
21:     domainEventPublisher.publish(Restaurant.class, restaurant.getId(), Collections.singletonList(new RestaurantCreated(request.getName(), request.getAddress(), request.getMenu())));
22:     return restaurant;
23:   }
24:   public Optional<Restaurant> findById(long restaurantId) {
25:     return restaurantRepository.findById(restaurantId);
26:   }
27: }

File: ftgo-restaurant-service-aws-lambda/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/restaurantservice/lambda/AbstractAutowiringHttpRequestHandler.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.lambda;
 2: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.Context;
 3: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.events.APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws.AbstractHttpHandler;
 5: import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
 6: import org.springframework.context.ApplicationContext;
 7: import org.springframework.context.ConfigurableApplicationContext;
 8: import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantReadWriteLock;
 9: public abstract class AbstractAutowiringHttpRequestHandler extends AbstractHttpHandler {
10:   private static ConfigurableApplicationContext ctx;
11:   private ReentrantReadWriteLock ctxLock = new ReentrantReadWriteLock();
12:   private boolean autowired = false;
13:   protected synchronized ApplicationContext getAppCtx() {
14:     ctxLock.writeLock().lock();
15:     try {
16:       if (ctx == null) {
17:         ctx =  SpringApplication.run(getApplicationContextClass());
18:       }
19:       return ctx;
20:     } finally {
21:       ctxLock.writeLock().unlock();
22:     }
23:   }
24:   protected abstract Class<?> getApplicationContextClass();
25:   @Override
26:   protected void beforeHandling(APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent request, Context context) {
27:     super.beforeHandling(request, context);
28:     if (!autowired) {
29:       getAppCtx().getAutowireCapableBeanFactory().autowireBean(this);
30:       autowired = true;
31:     }
32:   }
33: }

File: ftgo-restaurant-service-aws-lambda/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/restaurantservice/lambda/CreateRestaurantRequestHandler.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.lambda;
 2: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.Context;
 3: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.events.APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent;
 4: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.events.APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent;
 5: import io.eventuate.common.json.mapper.JSonMapper;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws.ApiGatewayResponse;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.Restaurant;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.RestaurantService;
 9: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.CreateRestaurantRequest;
10: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.web.CreateRestaurantResponse;
11: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
12: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
13: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
14: import static net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws.ApiGatewayResponse.applicationJsonHeaders;
15: @Configuration
16: @Import(RestaurantServiceLambdaConfiguration.class)
17: public class CreateRestaurantRequestHandler extends AbstractAutowiringHttpRequestHandler {
18:   @Autowired
19:   private RestaurantService restaurantService;
20:   @Override
21:   protected Class<?> getApplicationContextClass() {
22:     return CreateRestaurantRequestHandler.class;
23:   }
24:   @Override
25:   protected APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent handleHttpRequest(APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent request, Context context) {
26:     CreateRestaurantRequest crr = JSonMapper.fromJson(request.getBody(), CreateRestaurantRequest.class);
27:     Restaurant rest = restaurantService.create(crr);
28:     return ApiGatewayResponse.builder()
29:             .setStatusCode(200)
30:             .setObjectBody(new CreateRestaurantResponse(rest.getId()))
31:             .setHeaders(applicationJsonHeaders())
32:             .build();
33:   }
34: }

================
File: ftgo-restaurant-service-aws-lambda/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/restaurantservice/lambda/FindRestaurantRequestHandler.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.lambda;
 2: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.Context;
 3: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.events.APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent;
 4: import com.amazonaws.services.lambda.runtime.events.APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent;
 5: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws.ApiGatewayResponse;
 6: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws.AwsLambdaError;
 7: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.Restaurant;
 8: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.RestaurantService;
 9: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
10: import java.util.Optional;
11: import static net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws.ApiGatewayResponse.applicationJsonHeaders;
12: import static net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.aws.ApiGatewayResponse.buildErrorResponse;
13: public class FindRestaurantRequestHandler extends AbstractAutowiringHttpRequestHandler {
14:   @Autowired
15:   private RestaurantService restaurantService;
16:   @Override
17:   protected Class<?> getApplicationContextClass() {
18:     return CreateRestaurantRequestHandler.class;
19:   }
20:   @Override
21:   protected APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent handleHttpRequest(APIGatewayProxyRequestEvent request, Context context) {
22:     long restaurantId;
23:     try {
24:       restaurantId = Long.parseLong(request.getPathParameters().get("restaurantId"));
25:     } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
26:       return makeBadRequestResponse(context);
27:     }
28:     Optional<Restaurant> possibleRestaurant = restaurantService.findById(restaurantId);
29:     return possibleRestaurant
30:             .map(this::makeGetRestaurantResponse)
31:             .orElseGet(() -> makeRestaurantNotFoundResponse(context, restaurantId));
32:   }
33:   private APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent makeBadRequestResponse(Context context) {
34:     return buildErrorResponse(new AwsLambdaError(
35:             "Bad response",
36:             "400",
37:             context.getAwsRequestId(),
38:             "bad response"));
39:   }
40:   private APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent makeRestaurantNotFoundResponse(Context context, long restaurantId) {
41:     return buildErrorResponse(new AwsLambdaError(
42:                     "No entity found",
43:                     "404",
44:                     context.getAwsRequestId(),
45:                     "Found no restaurant with id " + restaurantId));
46:   }
47:   private  APIGatewayProxyResponseEvent makeGetRestaurantResponse(Restaurant restaurant) {
48:     return ApiGatewayResponse.builder()
49:                     .setStatusCode(200)
50:                     .setObjectBody(new GetRestaurantResponse(restaurant.getName()))
51:                     .setHeaders(applicationJsonHeaders())
52:                     .build();
53:   }
54: }

 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.web;
 2: public class CreateRestaurantResponse {
 3:   private long id;

File: ftgo-restaurant-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/restaurantservice/domain/RestaurantService.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.events.RestaurantCreated;
 3: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 4: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
 5: import java.util.Collections;
 6: import java.util.Optional;
 7: @Transactional
 8: public class RestaurantService {
 9:   @Autowired
10:   private RestaurantRepository restaurantRepository;
11:   @Autowired
12:   private RestaurantDomainEventPublisher restaurantDomainEventPublisher;
13:   public Restaurant create(CreateRestaurantRequest request) {
14:     Restaurant restaurant = new Restaurant(request.getName(), request.getMenu());
15:     restaurantRepository.save(restaurant);
16:     restaurantDomainEventPublisher.publish(restaurant, Collections.singletonList(new RestaurantCreated(request.getName(), request.getAddress(), request.getMenu())));
17:     return restaurant;
18:   }
19:   public Optional<Restaurant> findById(long restaurantId) {
20:     return restaurantRepository.findById(restaurantId);
21:   }
22: }

================
File: ftgo-restaurant-service/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/restaurantservice/web/RestaurantController.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.web;
 2: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.Restaurant;
 3: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.RestaurantService;
 4: import net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.restaurantservice.domain.CreateRestaurantRequest;
 5: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 6: import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
 7: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
 8: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
 9: @RestController
10: @RequestMapping(path = "/restaurants")
11: public class RestaurantController {
12:   @Autowired
13:   private RestaurantService restaurantService;
14:   @RequestMapping(method = RequestMethod.POST)
15:   public CreateRestaurantResponse create(@RequestBody CreateRestaurantRequest request) {
16:     Restaurant r = restaurantService.create(request);
17:     return new CreateRestaurantResponse(r.getId());
18:   }
19:   @RequestMapping(method = RequestMethod.GET, path = "/{restaurantId}")
20:   public ResponseEntity<GetRestaurantResponse> get(@PathVariable long restaurantId) {
21:     return restaurantService.findById(restaurantId)
22:             .map(r -> new ResponseEntity<>(makeGetRestaurantResponse(r), HttpStatus.OK))
23:             .orElseGet(() -> new ResponseEntity<>(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND));
24:   }
25:   private GetRestaurantResponse makeGetRestaurantResponse(Restaurant r) {
26:     return new GetRestaurantResponse(r.getId(), r.getName());
27:   }
28: }

================
File: ftgo-test-util-json-schema/src/main/java/net/chrisrichardson/ftgo/testutil/jsonschema/ValidatingJSONMapper.java
================
 1: package net.chrisrichardson.ftgo.testutil.jsonschema;
 2: import io.eventuate.common.json.mapper.JSonMapper;
 3: import org.apache.commons.lang.StringUtils;
 4: import org.everit.json.schema.Schema;
 5: import org.everit.json.schema.ValidationException;
 6: import org.everit.json.schema.loader.SchemaClient;
 7: import org.everit.json.schema.loader.SchemaLoader;
 8: import org.json.JSONException;
 9: import org.json.JSONObject;
10: import org.json.JSONTokener;
11: import org.junit.Assert;
12: import java.io.IOException;
13: import java.io.InputStream;
14: import static org.junit.Assert.fail;
15: public class ValidatingJSONMapper {
16:   private Schema schema;
17:   public ValidatingJSONMapper(Schema schema) {
18:     this.schema = schema;
19:   }
20:   public static ValidatingJSONMapper forSchema(String schemaPath) {
21:     Schema schema;
22:     try (InputStream inputStream = ValidatingJSONMapper.class.getResourceAsStream(schemaPath)) {
23:       if (inputStream == null)
24:         fail("Can't find schema: " + schemaPath);
25:       JSONObject rawSchema = new JSONObject(new JSONTokener(inputStream));
26:       schema = SchemaLoader.load(rawSchema, new SchemaClient() {
27:         @Override
28:         public InputStream get(String url) {
29:           String path = StringUtils.substringBeforeLast(schemaPath, "/") + "/" + url;
30:           InputStream is = ValidatingJSONMapper.class.getResourceAsStream(path);
31:           Assert.assertNotNull(path, is);
32:           return is;
33:         }
34:       });
35:     } catch (IOException | JSONException e) {
36:       throw new RuntimeException(e);
37:     }
38:     return new ValidatingJSONMapper(schema);
39:   }
40:   public void validate(JSONObject jsonObject) {
41:     try {
42:       schema.validate(jsonObject);
43:     } catch (ValidationException e) {
44:       e.getErrorMessage();
45:       fail("Schema validation failed: " + String.join(",", e.getAllMessages()));
46:     }
47:   }
48:   public void validate(String jsonObject) {
49:     JSONObject jo = new JSONObject(new JSONTokener(jsonObject));
50:     validate(jo);
51:   }
52:   public <T> T fromJSON(JSONObject jsonObject, Class<T> clasz) {
53:     validate(jsonObject);
54:     return JSonMapper.fromJson(jsonObject.toString(), clasz);
55:   }
56:   public String toJSON(Object object) {
57:     String json = JSonMapper.toJson(object);
58:     validate(json);
59:     return json;
60:   }
61: }
